  1. [2609.22263] 关于Kenmotsu流形的部分反不变子流形的一个注记
    A Note on Partially Anti-invariant Submanifolds of Kenmotsu Manifolds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Kenmotsu流形中部分反不变子流形的几何性质。
    • 考察分布可积性、全测地叶状结构及结构张量平行时的性质。
    • 利用曲率张量和Kenmotsu空间形式导出几何特征。
    • 刻画全脐情形并建立不等式及等号条件。
    💡 研究Kenmotsu流形中部分反不变子流形的可积性、全测地叶状结构及曲率特征，并给出不等式与等号情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文引入并研究Kenmotsu流形的部分反不变子流形。考察了相关分布的可积性，获得了保证诱导叶状结构为全测地的条件。进一步探讨了当结构张量N和P平行时这些分布的几何性质。利用Kenmotsu流形的曲率张量以及具有常psi截面曲率的Kenmotsu空间形式的性质，导出了部分反不变子流形的多种几何特征。此外，刻画了全脐部分反不变子流形，并建立了Kenmotsu空间形式中部分反不变子流形的一个不等式，包括等号情形的刻画。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we introduce and investigate partially antiinvariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu manifolds. The integrability of the involved distributions is examined, and conditions ensuring that the induced foliations are totally geodesic are obtained. We further explore the geometric properties of these distributions when the associated structure tensors N and P are parallel. By making use of the curvature tensor of Kenmotsu manifolds and the properties of Kenmotsu space forms with constant psi sectional curvature, we derive various geometric characterizations of partially anti-invariant submanifolds. In addition, we characterize totally umbilical partially anti-invariant submanifolds and establish an inequality for partially anti invariant submanifolds of Kenmotsu space forms, including the characterization of the equality case.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22263
  2. [2609.22313] Schwarz原始曲面椭圆参数化中参数的消去
    Elimination of the parameters in an elliptic parametrization of the Schwarz primitive surface
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Schwarz原始曲面椭圆参数化中参数的消去问题。
    • 利用椭圆函数构造参数化并确定精确归一化常数。
    • 证明关键恒等式(★)并展示其加性可分离性。
    • 为曲面参数化提供代数消去方法。
    💡 通过消去参数证明Schwarz原始曲面参数化满足的加性可分离恒等式，并给出精确归一化常数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设Ω为由四条圆弧|ζ∓1|=√2, |ζ∓i|=√2围成的曲线正方形，W=√(1+14ζ^4+ζ^8)。定义θ(ζ)=arcsin(2(1+i)ζ/√(1+4iζ^2-ζ^4))，f=1/4(-iF[θ,1/4]+F[θ,3/4])，g=1/4(iF[θ,1/4]+F[θ,3/4])，𝔥=(2-√3)F[arcsin(i(2+√3)ζ^2),(2-√3)^4]。这些正是我们关于菱形曲面D的姊妹论文[D]中的三个函数：两文起点相同，仅取实部不同。令X=(κ Re f, κ Re g, 1/2+κ Im 𝔥)，κ=3/(2K[1/9])，则映射X参数化Schwarz原始曲面P的基本拼块，P是D的共轭。第一部分建立代数工具……归一化常数被精确确定：ϖ:=κ^{-1}=K[-3]=1/2 K[3/4]=2/3 K[1/9]。第二部分执行消去并完成(★)的证明：sn(ϖ(x+y),-3) sn(ϖ(x-y),-3)=1/3 sn(3ϖ(z-1/2),1/9)。第三部分表明(★)是加性可分离的……关于本文和[D]的概述，请见arXiv:2609.14206。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Omega$ be the curvilinear square bounded by the four circular arcs $|\zeta\mp1|=\sqrt{2}$, $|\zeta\mp i|=\sqrt{2}$, and let $W=\sqrt{1+14\zeta^{4}+\zeta^{8}}$. Put \[ \theta(\zeta)=\arcsin\frac{2(1+i)\zeta} {\sqrt{1+4i\zeta^{2}-\zeta^{4}}}, \] \[ f=\tfrac14\bigl(-iF[\theta,\tfrac14]+F[\theta,\tfrac34]\bigr),\quad \quad g=\tfrac14\bigl(\ \ iF[\theta,\tfrac14]+F[\theta,\tfrac34]\bigr), \] \[ \mathfrak h=(2-\sqrt3)\, F\bigl[\arcsin\bigl(i(2+\sqrt3)\zeta^{2}\bigr),(2-\sqrt3)^{4}\bigr]. \] These are, verbatim, the three functions of our companion paper $\mathrm{[D]}$ on the diamond surface $\mathrm{D}$: the two papers start from the same point, and differ only in which real parts are taken. With $X=(\kappa\,\operatorname{Re}f,\ \kappa\,\operatorname{Re}g,\ \tfrac12+\kappa\,\operatorname{Im}\mathfrak{h})$ and $\kappa=3/(2K[1/9])$, the map $X$ parametrizes a fundamental patch of Schwarz's primitive surface $\mathrm{P}$, the conjugate of $\mathrm{D}$. Part I builds the algebraic apparatus $\ldots$ the normalizing constant is identified exactly: $\varpi:=\kappa^{-1}=K[-3]=\tfrac12 K[3/4]=\tfrac23 K[1/9]$. Part II carries out the elimination and completes the proof of \[ (\star)\qquad \operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{+}y),-3\bigr)\,\operatorname{sn}\bigl(\varpi(x{-}y),-3\bigr) =\tfrac13\operatorname{sn}\bigl(3\varpi(z-\tfrac12),\tfrac19\bigr). \] Part III shows that $(\star)$ is, after all, additively separable $\ldots$ for an overview of this paper and $\mathrm{[D]}$, please see arXiv:2609.14206.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22313
  3. [2609.22418] 带电初始数据集的次优Penrose不等式
    The suboptimal Penrose inequality for charged initial data sets
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究带电初始数据集的Penrose型不等式。
    • 证明质量下界包含面积和电荷项。
    • 推广到渐近双曲情形并替换为双曲能量。
    • 轴对称时进一步加入角动量项。
    💡 对带电渐近平坦初始数据集证明质量下界不等式，并推广到渐近双曲情形及轴对称含角动量情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    给定一个完备的3维渐近平坦的Einstein-Maxwell方程初始数据集，且磁场为零，我们证明存在一个小的通用常数C，使得m≥C(√(A/(16π))+Q^2√(π/A))。对于渐近双曲初始数据集，类似的结论成立，其中ADM质量替换为双曲能量。在额外的轴对称假设下，该不等式可进一步加强以包含角动量。这些结果将Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri最近获得的Penrose型不等式推广到带电情形。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Given a complete, 3-dimensional, asymptotically flat initial data set for the Einstein-Maxwell equations with vanishing magnetic field, we show that there exists a small universal constant $\mathcal{C}$ such that $m\geq\mathcal{C}(\sqrt{\mathcal{A}/(16\pi)}+\mathcal{Q}^2\sqrt{\pi/\mathcal{A}}\,)$. An analogous statement holds for asymptotically hyperboloidal initial data sets with the ADM mass replaced by hyperbolic energy. Under the additional assumption of axisymmetry, the inequality may be further strengthened to include angular momentum. These results extend the Penrose-type inequalities recently obtained by Allen-Bryden-Kazaras-Khuri to the charged setting.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22418
  4. [2609.22447] 余维2中varifolds的尖锐等周不等式
    The sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds in codimension two
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究余维2中varifolds的等周不等式。
    • 证明Michael-Simon不等式并推出尖锐等周不等式。
    • 等号仅在圆盘时成立。
    • 证明采用最优传输方法。
    💡 证明余维2中varifolds的Michael-Simon不等式，并推出尖锐等周不等式，等号仅在圆盘时成立。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们为欧几里得空间中的一类varifolds证明了Michael-Simon不等式，该类包含所有具有局部有界第一变差的任意维数和余维的整varifolds。在余维2中，该不等式是尖锐的，并蕴含varifolds的尖锐等周不等式，等号仅在圆盘时成立。我们的证明使用了Brendle-Eichmair的最优传输方法。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a Michael-Simon inequality for a class of varifolds in Euclidean space that includes all integral varifolds with locally bounded first variation of arbitrary dimension and codimension. In codimension $2$, this inequality is sharp and implies the sharp isoperimetric inequality for varifolds, which attains equality precisely for the round disk. Our proof uses the optimal transportation approach of Brendle-Eichmair.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22447
  5. [2609.22459] 风险最优资产再配置的微分几何框架
    A Differential-Geometric Framework for Risk-Optimal Asset Reallocation
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 提出微分几何框架用于风险最优资产再配置。
    • 将再平衡轨迹建模为黎曼度量下的测地线。
    • 比较测地线与线性再平衡及梯度上升路径。
    • 数值实验表明拥挤区域可带来最大风险节省。
    💡 用微分几何框架将投资组合再配置建模为测地线，比较不同路径并数值验证拥挤区域可带来风险节省。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们开发了一个用于风险最优投资组合再配置的微分几何框架。将只做多投资组合表示为概率单纯形上的点，赋予一个正定黎曼度量，该度量结合了市场协方差风险与依赖于头寸的集中风险。再平衡轨迹的累积风险被等同于其黎曼长度，因此从当前配置到Markowitz目标的最小风险转移是一条测地线。我们将此路径与直接线性再平衡和投影梯度上升进行比较。当仅对市场协方差风险定价时，度量为常值且平坦，测地线恰好是直线路径。一旦引入依赖于头寸的风险，几何变为弯曲，测地线弱支配具有相同端点的竞争路径。Fisher-Rao集中项产生适度但系统的节省，而内生拥挤度量产生非凸风险脊，测地线可通过临时分散化绕过。数值实验、蒙特卡洛转移和回归分析表明，当直接路径穿过强拥挤区域时收益最大。该框架为转移管理提供了一般的几何表述，并可容纳更丰富的风险度量和交易成本结构。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop a differential-geometric framework for risk-optimal portfolio reallocation. A long-only portfolio is represented as a point of the probability simplex, endowed with a positive-definite Riemannian metric combining market covariance risk with position-dependent concentration risk. The cumulative risk of a rebalancing trajectory is identified with its Riemannian length, so the least-risk transition between a current allocation and a Markowitz target is a geodesic. We compare this route with direct linear rebalancing and projected gradient ascent. When only market covariance risk is priced, the metric is constant and flat, and the geodesic is exactly the straight-line path. Once position-dependent risk is introduced, the geometry becomes curved and the geodesic weakly dominates competing paths with the same endpoints. A Fisher-Rao concentration term produces modest but systematic savings, while an endogenous crowding metric creates non-convex risk ridges that geodesics can bypass through temporary diversification. Numerical experiments, Monte Carlo transitions, and regression analysis show that the largest gains occur when the direct path crosses strongly crowded regions. The framework provides a general geometric formulation of transition management and can accommodate richer risk metrics and transaction-cost structures.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22459
  6. [2609.22565] 标准嵌入下物理异质G_2形变复形的链级解耦
    Chain-level decoupling of the physical heterotic $G_2$ deformation complex at the standard embedding
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究异质G_2紧化形变复形的链级解耦。
    • 在标准嵌入下构造显式同伦消除耦合。
    • 证明上同调分解为瞬子和几何部分。
    • 论证仅需完整群包含于G_2。
    💡 在标准嵌入下显式构造链级解耦，将异质G_2形变复形的上同调分解为瞬子与几何部分。
    📖 中文摘要

    异质G_2紧化的物理形变复形将瞬子形变与几何场及其一阶α'修正耦合。我们在最小标准嵌入下给出其一阶系数复形的显式链级解耦，其中规范丛为切丛，规范联络为Levi-Civita联络，背景G_2结构无挠。第一个Atiyah耦合通过一个扩展诱导联络变分的斜协变导数零伦。第二个同伦由取值于自同态的上链的斜部分的协变余微分给出，它消除了从丛扇区到几何扇区的耦合。剩余的几何曲率项与第一个变换的贡献相消。这些恒等式在每一正则次数的任意上链上成立，并产生互逆的微分算子链映射。因此，上同调在所有次数上分解为瞬子和几何系数上同调。论证仅需Hol(g)⊆G_2。据我们所知，此前没有显式的全次数分裂能消除该物理约化标准嵌入系数复形的内部规范与几何耦合。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The physical deformation complex of heterotic $G_2$ compactifications couples instanton deformations to geometric fields and their first-order $\alpha'$ corrections. We give an explicit chain-level decoupling of its first-order coefficient complex at the minimal standard embedding, where the gauge bundle is the tangent bundle, the gauge connection is the Levi--Civita connection, and the background $G_2$ structure is torsion-free. The first Atiyah coupling is null-homotopic through a skew covariant derivative extending the induced connection variation. A second homotopy, given by the covariant codifferential of the skew part of an endomorphism-valued cochain, removes the coupling from the bundle sector to the geometric sector. The residual geometric curvature term cancels against the contribution of the first transformation. These identities hold on arbitrary cochains in every canonical degree and yield mutually inverse differential-operator chain maps. Consequently, the cohomology decomposes into instanton and geometric coefficient cohomologies in all degrees. The argument requires only $\mathrm{Hol}(g)\subseteq G_2$. To our knowledge, no previous explicit all-degree splitting removes the internal gauge and geometric couplings of this physically reduced standard-embedding coefficient complex.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22565
  7. [2609.22575] 弹性BB纽结是多重的圆
    Elastic BB knots are multifold circles
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究弹性纽结在BB纽结类中的分类。
    • 假设广义Fáry-Milnor不等式成立。
    • 证明弹性纽结是圆的多重覆盖。
    • 通过收缩闭辫和变分方法完成证明。
    💡 在广义Fáry-Milnor不等式假设下，证明BB纽结类的弹性纽结是圆的多重覆盖。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究通过最小化单位环的弯曲能量与ropelength的小倍数之和得到的弹性纽结。我们假设纽结类的C^1边界上有一个广义Fáry-Milnor不等式：C^1闭包中的每个H^2极限曲线具有至少2π倍桥指标的总曲率。在此假设下，我们证明一组件BB纽结类（即桥指标与辫指标相等的类）中的每个弹性纽结是恰好覆盖该次数次的圆。证明不区分非光滑的ropelength泛函。一个极小闭辫在实心环的纤维中被收缩，产生一族具有二次弯曲能量超出和线性阶厚度的比较曲线。这给出了正则化极小值的厚度下界。中间尺度上的变分然后给出极限曲线对归一化弯曲能量的自由驻定性。常曲率和自由驻定性将分类归结为一个线性分布常微分方程。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study elastic knots obtained by minimizing the bending energy of a unit loop together with a small multiple of ropelength. We assume a generalized F\'ary-Milnor inequality on the $C^1$-boundary of a knot class: every $H^2$-limit curve in the $C^1$-closure has total curvature at least $2\pi$ times the bridge index. Under this assumption we prove that every elastic knot in a one-component BB knot class, that is, a class whose bridge and braid indices coincide, is the round circle covered precisely that many times. The proof does not differentiate the nonsmooth ropelength functional. A minimal closed braid is contracted in the fibres of a solid torus, producing a family of comparison curves with quadratic bending-energy excess and thickness of linear order. This yields a lower thickness bound for the regularized minimizers. Variations on an intermediate scale then give free stationarity of the limiting curve for the normalized bending energy. Constant curvature and free stationarity reduce the classification to a linear distributional ordinary differential equation.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22575
  8. [2609.22659] CP^2上第一Dirichlet特征函数的对数凹性
    Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions on $\mathbb{CP}^2$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究CP^2中1-凸域上第一Dirichlet特征函数的对数凹性。
    • 证明定量估计并推出严格对数凹。
    • 得到水平集凸性估计和谱隙下界。
    💡 证明CP^2中1-凸域上第一Dirichlet特征函数严格对数凹，并给出凸性估计和谱隙下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究CP^2中区域上第一Dirichlet特征函数的对数凹性。对于每个光滑1-凸域Ω⊂CP^2，我们证明其第一Dirichlet特征函数u满足定量估计∇^2(-log u) > max{ψ(s), 8/5}g，其中ψ(|∇f|^2)=s/√(1+s)-log(1+s)。特别地，u是严格对数凹的。作为推论，我们得到u的正则水平集的均匀凸性估计以及基本谱隙界λ_2-λ_1>46/5。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the log-concavity property of first Dirichlet eigenfunctions on domains in $\mathbb{CP}^2$. For every smooth $1$-convex domain $\Omega\subset\mathbb{CP}^2$, we prove the quantitative estimate \[ \nabla^2(-\log u) > \max\left\{\psi(s),\frac85\right\}g, \text{ where } \psi(|\grad f|^2) = \frac{s}{\sqrt{1+s}}-\log(1+s), \] for its first Dirichlet eigenfunction $u$. In particular, $u$ is strictly log-concave. As consequences, we obtain a uniform convexity estimate for the regular level sets of $u$ and the fundamental gap bound $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>46/5$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22659
  9. [2609.22676] 非负曲率黎曼流形子流形上的Pólya-Szegő不等式及应用
    P\'olya--Szeg\"o Inequality on Submanifolds of Riemannian Manifolds with Nonnegative Curvature and Applications
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究非负曲率流形子流形上的Pólya-Szegő不等式。
    • 常数依赖平均曲率和等周量。
    • 推出Sobolev、Hardy等不等式及临界情形结果。
    • 在特定条件下常数与欧几里得尖锐常数一致。
    💡 在非负曲率流形子流形上证明Pólya-Szegő不等式，并推出Sobolev、Hardy等不等式及临界情形结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们为定义在完备非紧非负截面曲率黎曼流形的n维子流形Σ上的函数证明了Pólya-Szegő不等式。相关的重排是R^n上的Schwarz重排，常数依赖于Σ的平均曲率的L^n范数以及由Brendle得到的等周量。作为应用，我们在小总平均曲率假设下，推导了任意余维子流形上的Sobolev、Log-Sobolev、Hardy和Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式。在临界Sobolev情形，我们获得了有限体积子流形上的Moser-Trudinger不等式和无限体积子流形上的精确增长不等式。在适当假设下，Pólya-Szegő常数等于1；此时，不等式中的临界常数与尖锐欧几里得常数一致。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a P\'olya--Szeg\"o inequality for functions defined on an $n$-dimensional submanifold $\Sigma$ of a complete noncompact Riemannian manifold with nonnegative sectional curvature. The associated rearrangement is a Schwarz rearrangement on $\mathbb R^n$, and the constant depends on the $L^n$-norm of the mean curvature of $\Sigma$ and an isoperimetric quantity obtained by Brendle. As applications, we derive Sobolev, Log-Sobolev, Hardy, and Gagliardo--Nirenberg inequalities on submanifolds of arbitrary codimension under a small total mean curvature assumption. In the critical Sobolev case, we obtain Moser--Trudinger inequalities on finite-volume submanifolds and exact growth inequalities on submanifolds with infinite volume. Under suitable assumptions, the P\'olya--Szeg\"o constant equals one; in this case, the critical constants in the inequalities coincide with the sharp Euclidean ones.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22676
  10. [2609.22832] 射影诱导的Kähler-Einstein曲面
    Projectively induced K\"ahler--Einstein surfaces
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 分类射影诱导的紧Kähler-Einstein曲面。
    • 证明仅有CP^2和CP^1×CP^1两种。
    • 通过反规范根构造和秩估计排除Fano指标一分支。
    • 推论此类曲面均为齐性。
    💡 分类由射影空间全纯等距浸入诱导的紧Kähler-Einstein曲面，证明仅有CP^2和CP^1×CP^1。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们分类了由到有限维复射影空间的全纯等距浸入诱导度量的紧Kähler-Einstein曲面。没有对称性假设，也没有余维数限制。我们证明这样的曲面仅有(CP^2, m g_FS)和(CP^1×CP^1, m(g_FS⊕g_FS))，m∈Z_{>0}，分别由Veronese和Segre-Veronese嵌入实现。主要新要素是对整个Fano指标一分支的余维无关排除，结合了公共反规范根构造与Gram-Gauss秩估计，并在次数五时使用等变曲率论证。因此，每个由到有限维复射影空间的全纯等距浸入诱导度量的连通紧Kähler-Einstein曲面都是齐性的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We classify compact K\"ahler--Einstein surfaces whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space. No symmetry assumption and no bound on the codimension are imposed. We prove that the only such surfaces are \[ (\PP^2,m g_{\FS}) \quad\text{and}\quad \bigl(\PP^1\times\PP^1,m(g_{\FS}\oplus g_{\FS})\bigr), \qquad m\in\mathbb Z_{>0}, \] realized respectively by the Veronese and Segre--Veronese embeddings. The main new ingredient is a codimension-independent exclusion of the entire Fano-index-one branch, combining a common anticanonical root construction with Gram--Gauss rank estimates and, in degree five, an equivariant curvature argument. Consequently, every connected compact K\"ahler--Einstein surface whose metric is induced by a holomorphic isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional complex projective space is homogeneous.

  11. [2609.23172] T^m上临界维数的自由映射
    Free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^m$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究环面上临界维数自由映射的存在性。
    • 通过乘积构造和交叉自由映射归纳。
    • 利用四元数乘法构造显式例子。
    • 证明对所有m≥1存在自由映射。
    💡 证明对任意m≥1，T^m到R^{q_m}存在临界维数的自由映射，通过乘积构造和四元数乘法归纳。
    📖 中文摘要

    m维流形上自由映射的临界维数为q_m=m(m+3)/2。我们证明对每个m≥1，Free^∞(T^m, R^{q_m})≠∅。主要工具是乘积构造：给定T^a和T^b上临界维数的自由映射，以及T^a×T^b上的交叉自由映射，其混合Hessian处处可逆，我们得到T^{a+b}上临界维数的自由映射，乘积的密切矩阵为块三角。交叉自由映射对每个自变量是加性的，在环面情形下，当一个因子是一维时不存在。我们利用四元数乘法在T^2×T^2和T^3×T^4上构造了显式交叉自由映射。结合作者在先前出版物中显式构造的低维情形m≤5，通过归纳得到结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The critical dimension for free maps on m-manifolds is $q_m=m(m+3)/2$. We show that $Free^\infty(\mathbb{T}^m,\mathbb{R}^{q_m})\neq\emptyset$ for every $m\ge 1$. The main tool is a product construction: given free maps in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^a$ and on $\mathbb{T}^b$, together with a cross-free map on $\mathbb{T}^a\times \mathbb{T}^b$, whose mixed Hessian is invertible everywhere, we obtain a free map in critical dimension on $\mathbb{T}^{a+b}$, the osculating matrix of the product being block-triangular. Cross-free maps are additive in each argument, and in the torus setting none exists when one factor is one-dimensional. We construct explicit cross-free maps on $\mathbb{T}^2\times \mathbb{T}^2$ and on $\mathbb{T}^3\times\mathbb{T}^4$ using quaternionic multiplication. Combined with the low-dimensional cases $m\leq 5$, constructed explicitly by the author in a previous publication, these yield the result by induction.

  12. [2609.23227] E(κ,τ)中满足Cauchy-Riemann不等式的球面及非CMC Hopf环面
    Spheres under a Cauchy-Riemann inequality in $E(\kappa,\tau)$, and non-CMC Hopf tori
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究E(κ,τ)中球面的Cauchy-Riemann不等式。
    • 证明弱不等式迫使平均曲率为常数。
    • 构造非CMC环面说明拓扑假设尖锐。

    💡 证明E(κ,τ)中球面满足弱Cauchy-Riemann不等式则平均曲率常值，并构造非CMC环面说明拓扑假设尖锐。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于光滑、连通、有向的拓扑球面Σ，浸入在τ≠0的齐性空间E(κ,τ)中，我们证明对原始Abresch-Rosenberg微分Q_AR的一个单侧Cauchy-Riemann型不等式——比要求Q_AR全纯弱得多——已经迫使平均曲率H为常数。证明对所有E(κ,τ)统一，包括圆球面，结合了Bers-Vekua相似原理和线场的Poincaré-Hopf指标公式；它在逻辑上独立于姊妹论文[AlencarRosenberg2026]中仅用Q_AR全纯性刻画CMC浸入的代数论证。我们还证明拓扑假设是尖锐的：在每个Berger球面（包括圆球面）上，存在紧非CMC环面——Hopf型淹没π:E(κ,τ)→M^2(κ)下基中具有非常值测地曲率的简单闭曲线的原像——满足相同不等式且常数有界。作为线场经典Poincaré-Hopf定理的基本推论，我们记录到每个det S<0处处成立的闭曲面——特别是每个Hopf管，其中det S≡-τ^2——是环面。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a smooth, connected, oriented surface $\Sigma$, topologically a sphere, immersed in the homogeneous space $E(\kappa,\tau)$ with $\tau\ne0$, we show that a one-sided Cauchy--Riemann-type inequality on the original Abresch--Rosenberg differential $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ -- much weaker than requiring $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ to be holomorphic -- already forces the mean curvature $H$ to be constant. The proof is uniform across every $E(\kappa,\tau)$, including the round sphere, and combines the Bers--Vekua similarity principle with the Poincar\'e-Hopf index formula for line fields; it is logically independent of the algebraic argument used, in the companion paper~\cite{AlencarRosenberg2026}, to characterize CMC immersions by holomorphy of $\mathcal{Q}_{AR}$ alone. We also show that the topological hypothesis is sharp: on every Berger sphere, including the round one, there exist compact non-CMC tori -- the preimages under the Hopf-type submersion $\pi:E(\kappa,\tau)\to M^2(\kappa)$ of simple closed curves in the base with nonconstant geodesic curvature -- satisfying the same inequality with a constant bound. As an elementary consequence of the classical Poincar\'e-Hopf theorem for line fields, we record that every closed surface on which $\det S<0$ everywhere -- a condition satisfied, in particular, by every Hopf tube, where $\det S\equiv-\tau^2$ -- is a torus.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入一种几何结构——共形Killing-Yano Ricci孤子（CKY-RS）——将共形Ricci孤子（CRS）几何与共形Killing-Yano（CKY）2-形式耦合。CRS几何的孤子场由CKY 2-形式的散度给出。我们引入守恒的CKY-Cotton流，并推导出联系Cotton张量、CRS障碍张量和CKY 2-形式的相容恒等式。在4维Lorentz号差中，我们证明在CKY形式的非退化性和闭性假设下，CKY-RS结构迫使共形代表局部为Kerr-NUT-(A)dS。对于Kerr-NUT-(A)dS背景上的闭非退化CKY，共形形变必为平凡的。对于任意维数和号差的Einstein背景，共形因子满足特征值方程和Obata型Hessian方程。若背景也是紧的或CKY轨道是周期的，则共形因子是CKY-流的不变量，我们得到非平凡CKY-RS结构的简单谱障碍。从Hessian方程，我们得到非平凡共形扇区的障碍和分类结果，包括乘积/Brinkmann几何和Weyl对齐分支。最后，我们给出静态球对称几何和BTZ背景的显式构造，包括一个具有时间依赖共形平坦代表的CKY-RS实现。这些结果为研究具有隐藏对称结构的CRS提供了几何框架，并可能应用于广义相对论中的精确几何。

    🌐 English Abstract

  24. [2609.23597] Rigidity of Einstein Manifolds under Two Eigenvalue Conditions on the Curvature Operator of the Second Kind
    Rigidity of Einstein Manifolds under Two Eigenvalue Conditions on the Curvature Operator of the Second Kind
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a rigidity theorem for closed Einstein manifolds of dimension $n\ge6$ under two lower bounds on the curvature operator of the second kind. More precisely, for $L_1, L_2\ge0$ and some real number $\alpha>1$ in a suitable range, we consider \[ \lambda_1\ge-L_1\bar\lambda, \qquad \frac1\alpha\sum_{j=1}^{\alpha}\lambda_j \ge-L_2\bar\lambda. \] Here $\lambda_1\le\cdots\le\lambda_N$ are the eigenvalues of the curvature operator of the second kind $\mathring R$, $N=(n-1)(n+2)/2$, and $\bar\lambda=N^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^N\lambda_j$. Let $\theta(n, \alpha)$ be the constant appearing in \cite[Theorem~1.1]{CW26}; see (2). In the parameter range considered here, $L_2>\theta(n, \alpha)$, so the second condition is strictly weaker than the corresponding condition in \cite{CW26}, while the first condition and that condition do not imply each other. Under an additional explicit relation between $L_1$ and $L_2$, we prove that the manifold is either flat or a spherical space form.

  25. [2609.23627] Convergence of cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian mean curvature flow in positive K\"ahler-Einstein manifolds
    Convergence of cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian mean curvature flow in positive K\"ahler-Einstein manifolds
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that Lagrangian mean curvature flow starting from a closed, embedded, cohomogeneity-one Lagrangian in a closed, positive K\"ahler--Einstein manifold exists for all time, remains embedded, and converges smoothly and graphically to a minimal Lagrangian, under natural exactness and regularity assumptions. We also study the generalised Lagrangian mean curvature flow of Behrndt in K\"ahler manifolds which are almost-Einstein in the sense that the Ricci form satisfies $\rho = C\omega + ndd^cf$, and which satisfy $C>0$. With analogous assumptions on the flow, we obtain subconvergence to an $f$-minimal Lagrangian submanifold, with an upgrade to smooth graphical convergence in the case that $(M,g,f)$ is analytic. The proof proceeds by first reducing the flow to a weighted curve shortening flow on a compact two-dimensional orbifold. We then establish both a Grayson-type description of finite-time singularities and a long-time subconvergence theorem for weighted curve shortening flow in the orbifold setting. Finally, we use a {\L}ojasiewicz--Simon inequality argument to upgrade to smooth convergence of the full flow.

    Blaschke Conjecture and Complex Geometry
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We provide a complex-geometric approach to the Blaschke conjecture, i.e., that a manifold whose injectivity radius equals its diameter is isometric to a compact rank-one symmetric space (CROSS). In particular, we introduce the great quadric fibration, a complex analogue of the great sphere fibration. Requiring its total space to be a complex submanifold simultaneously explains many properties that a Blaschke manifold is expected to possess, including its Clifford structure, Hopf fibration, and diffeomorphism class. Furthermore, the great quadric bundle coincides with the variety of minimal rational tangents (VMRT) of the ambient Fano variety in the standard CROSS cases, and we explain this through a bend-and-break argument in the setting of the Blaschke conjecture under a suitable complexification assumption. Combining this with Hwang-Mok VMRT recognition, we prove the Blaschke conjecture for manifolds admitting an adapted complex structure on their entire tangent bundles.

  30. [2609.24027] Weighted normalized curve shortening flow with applications in pseudo-Euclidean spaces
    Weighted normalized curve shortening flow with applications in pseudo-Euclidean spaces
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    The focus of this paper is the curve shortening flow for closed spacelike curves in pseudo-Euclidean spaces, which has very few results so far. Will they produce singularities where certain tangent line tends to light cone? If not, will such a curve shrink to a circular point? To answer these questions, we establish a dichotomy for planar weighted normalized curve shortening flow with uniformly positive and bounded weights. Applying to closed smooth spacelike curves in pseudo-Euclidean spaces that admit a one-to-one convex projection onto a spacelike plane, at their finite maximal time we will see: either the curve shrinks to a point and becomes asymptotically circular, or the tangent directions subsequentially approach the null cone. Both alternatives occur. In the first case, this proves our previous conjecture that a strong spacelike curve in $\mathbb{R}^{2,q}$ with index 1 will converge to a circular point under the usual CSF. In the latter case, a monotone area-bivector defect is found in $\mathbb R^{2,1}$, which gives a quantitative obstruction to point collapse. Explicit examples of spacelike curves with lightlike tangent limit are given.

  34. [2609.24406] Second Jacobi eigenvalues and spectral rigidity for surfaces in Berger spheres
    Second Jacobi eigenvalues and spectral rigidity for surfaces in Berger spheres
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we establish an upper bound for the second eigenvalue of the scalar Jacobi operator of an arbitrary closed two-sided surface immersed in a contracted Berger sphere. The estimate involves the total squared mean curvature, the Euler characteristic, and an explicit nonpositive term determined by the angle between the surface normal and the Hopf direction. The proof combines the realization of a Berger sphere as a geodesic hypersurface of a complex projective plane, the first standard embedding of the latter into a Euclidean sphere, a weighted Hersch--Li--Yau balancing argument, and the conformal invariance of the Willmore functional. No minimality or constant mean curvature assumption is imposed. As an application, if $1/3\leq\alpha\leq1$ and the surface has nonpositive Euler characteristic, then its second Jacobi eigenvalue is nonpositive; it is strictly negative for $\alpha>1/3$. At the critical value $\alpha=1/3$, equality forces the immersed image to be congruent to the minimal Clifford torus; in the embedded category, this yields a complete characterization of the equality case.

    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove sharp two-eigenvalue isoperimetric inequalities for Neumann and Aharonov--Bohm Neumann spectra on surfaces. If $\Omega\subset\mathbb S^2$ is smooth, simply connected and proper, then the harmonic mean of $\mu_2(\Omega)$ and $\mu_3(\Omega)$ is bounded above by the first positive Neumann eigenvalue of the equal-area geodesic disk, with equality only for disks. For simply connected surfaces with Gaussian curvature bounded above, we obtain the magnetic analogue for the first two Aharonov--Bohm eigenvalues. A key additional ingredient is a spectral ordering theorem for magnetic spherical caps: for $0<\nu<1/2$, the first two eigenvalues lie in the angular sectors of effective orders $\nu$ and $1-\nu$. We prove this by the factorization $L_0=T^*T$, $L_1=TT^*$ and an exact Neumann--Dirichlet spectral shift. We also obtain sharp full-sphere and closed-surface bounds, and an annular inequality in terms of conformal modulus and flux.

    • 在混合正交条件下，平均曲率向量分裂，复合极小当且仅当输入极小且轮廓W-极小。
    • 利用Hsiang-Lawson约化和Kapouleas-McGrath粘结构造闭嵌入W-极小轮廓。
    • 高斯转移从高斯种子产生内部轮廓，对极小曲面构造有重要意义。
    💡 建立了通过系数轮廓和保范双线性映射耦合的球面浸入的精确复合原理，并构造了闭嵌入W-极小轮廓。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立了通过系数轮廓和保范双线性映射耦合的球面浸入的精确复合原理。在显式混合正交条件下，球面平均曲率向量分裂为正交的输入和轮廓贡献。复合是极小的当且仅当每个输入是极小的且轮廓对显式单项式权重是W-极小的。利用Hsiang-Lawson约化和Kapouleas-McGrath粘合，我们构造了闭嵌入W-极小轮廓，包括具有无界中间Betti数的族。对于足够大的可比输入维数，本文开发的高斯转移从闭嵌入高斯种子产生内部轮廓，这些种子在旋转模下非退化。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish an exact composition principle for spherical immersions coupled through coefficient profiles and norm-preserving bilinear maps. Under an explicit mixed-orthogonality condition, the spherical mean-curvature vector splits into orthogonal input and profile contributions. The composition is minimal if and only if every input is minimal and the profile is $W$-minimal for an explicit monomial weight. Using Hsiang--Lawson reduction and Kapouleas--McGrath gluing, we construct closed embedded $W$-minimal profiles, including families with unbounded intermediate Betti numbers. For sufficiently large comparable input dimensions, a Gaussian transfer developed in this paper produces interior profiles from closed embedded Gaussian seeds that are nondegenerate modulo rotations.

  47. [2609.24909] White的Möbius带锥是面积最小的
    White's cone over the M\"obius band is area-minimising
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 关键是将柱面切片与R^3平坦秩一平移商中的极小曲面联系，对应非可定向极小螺旋面。
    • 证明锥在Z_2系数下局部质量最小，并分类满足柱面切片性质的极小超锥。
    • 对Plateau问题边界奇点模型有重要意义。
    💡 证明了R^4中适当极小Möbius带上的锥在Z_2系数下是局部质量最小的，证实了White的猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明R^4中适当极小Möbius带Σ⊂S^3上的锥C(Σ)在Z_2系数下是局部质量最小的。这证明了Brian White关于环境维数4或更高时Plateau问题边界奇点模型背景下的猜想。关键洞察是柱面切片C(Σ)∩{(z,w)∈R^2×R^2: |w|=r}是R^3的平坦秩一平移商中的极小曲面。事实上，它们对应于Antonio Ros先前研究的非可定向极小螺旋面。我们还证明，在刚性运动下，极小锥C(Σ)×R^{n-4}⊂R^n是唯一非平面的、边界为R^{n-2}、除边界外光滑且满足极小柱面切片性质的极小超锥。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that the cone $C(\Sigma)$ in $\mathbb R^4$ over a suitable minimal M\"obius band $\Sigma\subset \mathbb S^3$ is locally mass-minimising with coefficients in $\mathbb Z_2$. This proves a conjecture by Brian White in the context of models of boundary singularities of Plateau's problem in ambient dimension $4$ or higher. The key insight is that the cylindrical slices $C(\Sigma)\cap \{(z,w)\in \mathbb R^2 \times \mathbb R^2 : |w|=r\}$ are minimal surfaces in a flat rank-one translation quotient of $\mathbb R^3$. In fact, they correspond to non-orientable minimising helicoids previously studied by Antonio Ros. We also show that, up to rigid motions, the minimising cones $C(\Sigma)\times \mathbb R^{n-4}\subset \mathbb R^n$ are the only non-planar minimal hypercones with boundary $\mathbb R^{n-2}$ which are smooth away from the boundary that satisfy the minimal cylindrical slice property.

  48. [2609.24958] 一般面积最小超锥的定量不等式
    A quantitative inequality for general area-minimizing hypercones
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究一般面积最小超锥的定量稳定性不等式，允许非孤立奇点。
    • 采用校准论证，利用Zhihan Wang的极小叶状结构和投影核积分构造向量场。
    • 证明存在常数c_C使得周长差与对称差平方的定量不等式成立。
    • 推广了正则超锥的结果，对极小曲面稳定性理论有重要意义。
    💡 将正则面积最小超锥的无权重定量不等式推广到一般面积最小超锥，允许非孤立奇点。
    📖 中文摘要

    设C=∂E⊂R^{n+1}是面积最小超锥。该锥可能有非孤立奇点。我们证明存在常数c_C>0，使得对于所有R>0和所有局部有限周长集F满足F△E⋐B_R，有(Per(F;B_R)-Per(E;B_R))/R^n ≥ c_C (|F△E|/R^{n+1})^2。这将正则面积最小超锥的无权重定量不等式推广到一般面积最小超锥。我们遵循作者早期关于正则锥的工作中的校准论证。主要变化是向量场的构造。正则情形中使用的正Jacobi场的逐点渐近估计在此不直接适用。我们使用Zhihan Wang在锥两侧构造的极小叶状结构。在每个叶上，我们以正Jacobi场为权重积分投影核。核构造给出了所需的散度界。Wang的弱Harnack不等式和增长估计给出了证明向量场线性增长所需的积分界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\mathbf{C}=\partial E\subset\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ be an area-minimizing hypercone. The cone may have nonisolated singularities. We prove that there is a constant $c_{\mathbf{C}}>0$ such that \[ \frac{\operatorname{Per}(F;B_R)-\operatorname{Per}(E;B_R)}{R^n} \geq c_{\mathbf{C}} \left(\frac{|F\triangle E|}{R^{n+1}}\right)^2 \] for every $R>0$ and every set $F$ of locally finite perimeter with $F\triangle E\Subset B_R$. This extends the unweighted quantitative inequality from regular area-minimizing hypercones to general area-minimizing hypercones. We follow the calibration argument in the author's earlier work on regular cones. The main change is the construction of the vector fields. The pointwise asymptotic estimates for positive Jacobi fields used in the regular case do not directly apply here. We use the minimal foliations constructed by Zhihan Wang on the two sides of the cone. On each leaf, we integrate projection kernels with a positive Jacobi field as the weight. The kernel construction gives the required divergence bound. Wang's weak Harnack inequality and growth estimates give the integral bounds needed to prove linear growth of the vector fields.

  49. [2609.22279] 全纯叶状结构与曲线的几何指标定理
    Geometric index theorems for holomorphic foliations and curves
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究复曲面上全纯叶状结构相对于参考叶状结构和曲线的指标。
    • 定义相对指标并证明指标和等于T G·C，推广Camacho-Sad公式。
    • 得到两个接触公式，包括加权切点计数和总指标公式。
    • 对全纯叶状结构几何和射影不变量理论有重要意义。
    💡 为复曲面上的全纯叶状结构定义了相对指标，证明了指标和公式，并得到两个接触公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    设M是带有全纯射影联络的复曲面，C⊂M是紧光滑全纯曲线。给定C附近两个奇异全纯叶状结构F和G，且C不是G-不变的，我们为每点p∈C赋予F相对于参考G的指标Ind(F,G,C,p)∈C，并证明这些指标之和等于T G·C。当C是F-不变时，指标是Camacho-Sad指标经G与C的相切阶修正，指标公式退化为Camacho-Sad公式。主要应用是两个接触公式。在G与C的切点p处，设k(G,C,p)为G的叶与曲线在p处曲率之比；它是射影不变量，尽管每个曲率单独依赖于度量选择。若G在C上无奇点且与C仅有简单切触，则∑_p 1/(1-k(G,C,p)) = (2/3)(C·C + g - 1)，其中g是C的亏格：切点数依赖于G，但该加权计数不依赖。对于射影平面中的线束，这是平面曲线类的Plücker公式。第二个公式断言，对于关于F和G一般位置且不是测地线的曲线，F与G沿C的切触总指标等于G与C的切点数减去C的拐点数的三分之一；特别地，它不依赖于F。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $M$ be a complex surface endowed with a holomorphic projective connection and let $C \subset M$ be a compact smooth holomorphic curve. Given two singular holomorphic foliations $\mathcal{F}$ and $\mathcal{G}$ near $C$, with $C$ not $\mathcal{G}$-invariant, we attach to every point $p \in C$ an index $\mathrm{Ind}(\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G},C,p) \in \mathbb{C}$ of $\mathcal{F}$ relative to the reference $\mathcal{G}$, and we prove that these indices add up to $T\mathcal{G} \cdot C$. When $C$ is $\mathcal{F}$-invariant the index is the Camacho--Sad index corrected by the order of tangency of $\mathcal{G}$ with $C$, and the index formula reduces to the Camacho--Sad formula. The main applications are two contact formulas. At a tangency point $p$ of $\mathcal{G}$ with $C$, let $k(\mathcal{G},C,p)$ be the ratio of the curvatures at $p$ of the leaf of $\mathcal{G}$ and of the curve; it is a projective invariant, although each curvature separately depends on a choice of metric. If $\mathcal{G}$ has no singular points on $C$ and only simple tangencies with $C$, then $$ \sum_{p} \frac{1}{1 - k(\mathcal{G},C,p)} = \frac{2}{3}\left( C \cdot C + g - 1 \right), $$ where $g$ is the genus of $C$: the number of tangencies depends on $\mathcal{G}$, but this weighted count does not. For a pencil of lines in the projective plane it is the Pl\"ucker formula for the class of a plane curve. The second formula asserts that, for a curve in general position with respect to $\mathcal{F}$ and $\mathcal{G}$ which is not a geodesic, the total index of the tangencies of $\mathcal{F}$ with $\mathcal{G}$ along $C$ equals the number of tangencies of $\mathcal{G}$ with $C$ minus one third of the number of inflection points of $C$; in particular, it does not depend on $\mathcal{F}$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22279

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 实验显示最优收敛率，且比参数有限元更精确，对曲面PDE数值方法有重要意义。
    💡 利用共形球面样条求解亏格零曲面上的Poisson方程和Laplace-Beltrami特征值问题，仅以共形因子描述几何。
    📖 中文摘要

    每个光滑闭亏格零黎曼曲面都等距于带有共形度量的单位球面，即圆度量乘以正因子。本文用光滑球面样条求解此类度量的Laplace-Beltrami算子的Poisson方程和特征值问题，仅以共形因子作为几何描述。由于二维Dirichlet能量共形不变，刚度矩阵对每个共形度量都是圆球面矩阵，因子仅进入载荷向量、均值约束和加权质量矩阵。嵌入空间且具有共形参数化的曲面、旋转曲面以及仅以三角网格给出的曲面是同一问题的实例，区别在于因子的获取方式；前两者在此处理，且在离散化任何方程前用面积恒等式检验因子。未知量是固定球面三角剖分上的分片Bernstein-Bézier形式样条，通过边泛函代数地施加C^r光滑性，协调空间由一次计算并复用于每个度量的零空间矩阵实现。计算的每一步都给出所需公式。在圆度量、长球面、旋转哑铃、Evans-Fung红细胞轮廓和无可嵌入的指定因子上的实验显示了样条次数d的L^2收敛率d+1和能量收敛率d，特征值收敛率2d，且圆度量的偶数阶特征值复现到次数d的舍入误差，与相同三角剖分上的参数曲面有限元相比，在次数四和六时以相当未知量数获得更小误差。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Every smooth closed Riemannian surface of genus zero is isometric to the unit sphere carrying a conformal metric, the round metric multiplied by a positive factor. This paper solves the Poisson equation and the eigenvalue problem of the Laplace--Beltrami operator of such a metric with smooth spherical splines, taking the conformal factor as the only description of the geometry. Because the Dirichlet energy is conformally invariant in two dimensions, the stiffness matrix is the round-sphere matrix for every conformal metric, and the factor enters only the load vector, the mean-value constraint and the weighted mass matrix. A surface embedded in space with a conformal parametrization, a surface of revolution, and a surface given only as a triangle mesh are instances of the same problem that differ in how the factor is obtained; the first two are treated here, and the factor is checked against an area identity before any equation is discretized. The unknown is a spline on a fixed spherical triangulation in broken Bernstein--B\'ezier form, with $C^r$ smoothness imposed algebraically through edge functionals and the conforming space realized by a null-space matrix computed once and reused for every metric. Each step of the computation is stated with the formulas it needs. Experiments on the round metric, a prolate spheroid, a dumbbell of revolution, an Evans--Fung red-blood-cell profile and a prescribed factor with no embedding show the rates $d+1$ in $L^2$ and $d$ in energy for spline degree $d$, the rate $2d$ for eigenvalues with reproduction of the even-order eigenvalues of the round metric up to degree $d$ to roundoff, and, against parametric surface finite elements on the same triangulations, smaller errors at comparable numbers of unknowns for degrees four and six.

This leaves pair-adaptive use of graph geometry underexplored, which may limit adaptation to unseen entities in cold-start settings relevant to practical screening.     📖 中文摘要

    在本文中，我们详细证明了可解群S_{2,2+p}（度量等价于Calabi-Vesentini对称空间SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)×SO(2+p)）与支持齐次非对称特殊流形L(-1,p)的Kähler度量的可解群S_{L(-1,p)}之间的同构。从Alekseevsky的观点看，这两个空间对应于同一可解Lie代数上的两个不同二次型，我们详细展示并比较。进一步考虑L(-1,p)的完全等距群Iso_{L(-1,p)}，我们证明Iso_{L(-1,p)}⊂SO(2,2+p)是Calabi-Vesentini流形单等距群的非半单子群。总之，特殊Kähler流形L(-1,p)可视为陪集流形Iso_{L(-1,p)}/H，其中H=U(1)_L×SO(p)，U(1)_L的生成元在so(2,2+p)中但不在标准so(2)⊕so(2+p)子代数中。Iso_{L(-1,p)}在CV流形可解坐标上的作用可直接构造。这一识别为Cartan神经网络提供了有价值的工具，在基于CV Tits-Satake普适类的r=2神经网络的每层到下一层的映射中引入额外的非线性变换；有望增加表达能力。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper we prove in full detail the isomorphism between the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$, metric equivalent to the Calabi Vesentini symmetric space $\mathrm{SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)\times SO(2+p)}$, and the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ supporting the K\"ahler metric of the homogeneous non symmetric special manifold L$(-1,p)$. The action of $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ on the solvable coordinates of the CV manifold can be constructed directly. This identification provides a valuable tool for Cartan Neural Networks, introducing additional non linear transformations in every map from one layer to the next one of an $r=2$ Neural Network based on the CV Tits Satake universality class; in perspective, this new tool increases expressivity.

    • 得到L^p(1<p≤2)有界性，常数仅依赖p，无需几何假设。
    • 对调和分析和几何分析有重要意义。
    💡 证明了完备非紧黎曼流形上Riesz变换是弱(1,1)型，常数2，从而在L^p(1<p≤2)上有界，解决了Coulhon-Duong猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    设M是完备非紧黎曼流形。我们证明其Riesz变换是弱(1,1)型，对实值函数常数为2。因此，它在L^p(M)上有界（1<p≤2），常数仅依赖于p，这证明了Coulhon-Duong猜想。证明使用具有次马尔可夫半群的正自伴算子的障碍分解。将该分解应用于平移的平方根Laplacian并利用Sobolev微分的局部性，得到端点估计，无需几何或热核假设。我们还得到承认局部Hilbert微分计算的Dirichlet空间的相应结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $M$ be a complete, non-compact Riemannian manifold. We prove that its Riesz transform is of weak type $(1,1)$, with constant $2$ for real-valued functions. Consequently, it is bounded on $L^p(M)$ for $1<p\leq2$, with constants depending only on $p$, which proves the Coulhon--Duong conjecture. The proof uses an obstacle decomposition for positive self-adjoint operators with sub-Markovian semigroups. Applying this decomposition to the shifted square-root Laplacian and using locality of the Sobolev differential yields the endpoint estimate without geometric or heat kernel assumptions. We also obtain the corresponding result for Dirichlet spaces admitting a local Hilbertian differential calculus.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce determinantal ellipticity, or det-ellipticity, a quantitative structural condition for fully nonlinear elliptic operators on compact K\"ahler manifolds that provides the link between the $L^\infty$-estimates and algebraic/combinatorial properties of a large class of Hessian elliptic operators. On the analytic side, we introduce $\mathcal D$-subsolutions extending the determinant sublevel condition of Sui--Sun. This is equivalent to balanced-point conditions for the Newton polytopes of rank-one scalarizations and to slope semistability of an associated subspace polymatroid. Together with the analytic hypotheses, these criteria yield relative $L^\infty$ estimates for the corresponding fully nonlinear equations.

  59. [2609.24781] Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras from \v{S}evera bicomplexes
    Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras from \v{S}evera bicomplexes
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    A \v{S}evera bicomplex is a bicomplex $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ whose differentials $d_{i}$ are Grothendieck differential operators of order $\leq i$ with respect to a given $A$-module structure on $M$, where $A$ is a graded commutative algebra. One also requires that $(M,d_{0})$ admits a contracting homotopy $K$ that is a differential operator of the same order as $d_{0}$. Under suitable assumptions, the differential $d_{2}$ of the second page of the spectral sequence associated with $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ is a Batalin-Vilkovisky operator on $E_{2}$, so that when $E_{2}$ is a free rank 1 $A$-module, the graded commutative algebra structure of $A$ is enhanced to a Gerstenhaber algebra structure, and the choice of a $d_{2}$-closed basis element for $E_{2}$ further enhances this to a Batalin-Vilkovisky algebra structure. The prototypical example of this construction is \v{S}evera's description of the Batalin--Vilkovisky algebra structure on the algebra of smooth functions of an odd symplectic manifold. Throughout the whole article we look at Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras through the lenses of Cartan calculus. Indications of a generalization to derived Cartan calculus are briefly discussed in the concluding section.

  62. [2606.06619] On the structure of complete $G_2$-solitons
    On the structure of complete $G_2$-solitons
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    In this work, we establish compactness and regularity results for complete gradient Laplacian solitons of closed $G_2$-structures. Under a lower scalar-curvature bound and a distance-dependent bound on the gradient of the soliton potential, we prove pointed measured Gromov-Hausdorff compactness. With a uniform lower bound for the localised Perelman entropy, the Gromov-Hausdorff convergence improves to pointed $C^{1,\alpha}$ convergence. Our principal result shows that this \(C^{1,\alpha}\) convergence upgrades to smooth convergence on the regular set. More precisely, after passing to a subsequence, the metrics, the defining positive \(3\)-forms, and the soliton potentials converge smoothly on every compact subset of the regular set, and the limiting data define a gradient Laplacian soliton. The proof develops a local entropy method adapted to \(G_2\)-solitons. Since the available \(C^{1,\alpha}\) control does not directly close an elliptic bootstrap for the \(G_2\)-soliton, and no suitable pseudolocality theorem is available in this setting, we instead use the localised Perelman's functionals. These yield an entropy \(\varepsilon\)-regularity theorem and a gap theorem for scalar-flat solitons. On the regular set, pointed \(C^{1,\alpha}\) convergence gives an almost-Euclidean local isoperimetric inequality, which in turn verifies the required small-entropy condition automatically. The resulting curvature bounds are then combined with \(G_2\)-specific differential identities and quantitative interior estimates to control the soliton data. Finally, at the critical exponent in dimension seven, we show that a uniform weighted \(L^{\frac{7}{2}}\) -curvature bound then yields pointed $C^\infty$ compactness.

    Exponentiable Objects and Function spaces in Lowen Fuzzy Topological Spaces

Moreover, a classical space \(X\) is exponentiable exactly when its induced fuzzy space \(\omega X\) is exponentiable in the entire stratified Lowen category.     💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    A Stieltjes clock allows effective time to advance continuously, remain unchanged over intervals, or jump. We study whether a compact-valued set-valued map evolving relative to a Stieltjes clock admits a single-valued selection that passes through a prescribed graph point while retaining the regularity and variation of the multifunction. For g-H\"older exponents $\alpha \geq 1$, we prove that regularity can be preserved without increasing the g-H\"older seminorm, with no monotonicity assumption on g. If g is additionally nondecreasing, one prescribed-point selection preserves both this regularity and the Hausdorff variation on every subinterval. The same selection consequently preserves all finite Riesz p-variations associated with nondecreasing external clocks. For $\alpha>1$, the structure becomes jump-driven. For left-continuous nondecreasing Stieltjes clocks, continuous clock evolution cannot generate variation: all variation is carried by jumps. We obtain exact jump decompositions for both the set-valued map and its selection, together with explicit atomic formulas for Riesz p-variation. Examples show that the principal regularity, variation, and jump bounds are attained. For compact-convex Euclidean-valued maps, we also examine the case $0<\alpha<1$. In this setting, the H\"older exponent can still be preserved, but preservation of the same constant through a prescribed point holds in one dimension and can fail in higher dimensions. Finally, without a quantitative H\"older bound, we characterize exactly when every compact-valued Hausdorff g-continuous map admits a prescribed-point g-continuous selection: precisely when the clock image is zero-dimensional.

  12. [2609.24860] Trefoil Probabilities and Polyhedra
    Trefoil Probabilities and Polyhedra
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We determine the exact probability that the closed hexagonal polygon obtained by cyclically joining six independent points uniformly distributed on the unit sphere is knotted. The only possible nontrivial knot is a trefoil. Almost surely, the convex hull of the six points has one of two simplicial combinatorial types: a combinatorially regular octahedron and a combinatorially non-regular octahedron. We show that the straight-line complete graph on the six vertices of the hull of the combinatorially regular octahedral type admits exactly one unoriented trefoil Hamiltonian cycle. On the other hand, no cycle connecting vertices of the irregular octahedron can produce a trefoil. We then use stereographic projection to transform the probability of the regular hull type to a Sylvester-type problem for the planar beta-prime probability measure $d\mu(x,y)=\frac{dx dy}{\pi\ (1+x^2+y^2)^2}$. Applying Stokes' theorem and the Blaschke-Petkantschin formula, we compute the expected squared $\mu$-content of a random triangle. This yields a regular octahedral probability of $\frac{15}{4\pi^2}$ and a trefoil probability of $\frac{1}{16\pi^2}$.

  5. [2609.22380] A Machine-Checked Proof that Gardam's $\tilde{A}_2$ Lattice Does Not Have Unique Products
    A Machine-Checked Proof that Gardam's $\tilde{A}_2$ Lattice Does Not Have Unique Products
    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    Kaplansky's zero-divisor conjecture asserts that the group ring of a torsion-free group over a field has no zero divisors. It holds for every group with the unique-product property, so a counterexample can only come from a torsion-free group without unique products. In lectures in 2021, Gardam announced that the torsion-free $\tilde{A}_2$ lattice $\Gamma = \langle a, b \mid a b a^2 b^{-1} a^2 b^{-2}, a b^3 a b^4 a^{-1} b \rangle$ does not have unique products and presented it as a new candidate group: it has property (T), and the known methods for proving the conjecture do not apply to it. To our knowledge, no proof of the announcement has been published. We give a proof checked by the Lean 4 kernel and stated against Mathlib's UniqueProds class. The witness is an explicit pair of finite subsets with $|A| = 32$ and $|B| = 28$ in which each of the 896 products coincides with another product. For 658 products the certificate is an identity in the free group; the other 238 certificates are explicit products of conjugated relators, 970 conjugates in all, checked by free reduction. A homomorphism onto $\mathbb{Z}/42$ shows that each pair $(u,v)$ differs from its partner $(u',v')$ as a pair of group elements, which is all the theorem requires. Together with a homomorphism onto the alternating group $A_4$ it also shows that the listed words are pairwise distinct, so the sets have exactly 32 and 28 elements. The witness and certificates come from an untrusted search program and are re-checked by Lean. The development uses only the axioms propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound, with no sorry and no native_decide. The mathematical statement is Gardam's. To our knowledge this is the first verification in a proof assistant of a unique-product failure in a torsion-free group; torsion-freeness of $\Gamma$ is taken from Gardam and is not formalized here.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22380

    Boundary Kernel Rigidity and Infinite-Dimensional Complex Hyperbolic Representations
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究无限维复双曲空间的全纯等距群表示的分类问题。
    • 通过边界核的刚性定理，证明 Gram 矩阵正惯性指数条件。
    • 得到表示由参数 (t,ε) 分类，并证明 Monod 分类中的假设自动成立。
    • 该结果统一了已知端点刚性，并推广到全等距群的自表示。
    💡 证明边界核的刚性定理，并分类无限维复双曲空间的全纯等距群表示。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于 n≥2，我们证明了 S^{2n-1} 上 Hermitian 边界核 L_{t,s}(x,y)=|1-<x,y>|^t exp(is arg(1-<x,y>))（x≠y，L_{t,s}(x,x)=0，t>0，s∈[-1,1]）的刚性定理。L_{t,s} 的每个有限 Gram 矩阵的正惯性指数至多为 1 当且仅当 0<t≤1 且 s=±t。证明结合了边界圆上的傅里叶分析与到两个正交复方向的限制。维数阈值是尖锐的。通过规范化等变边界映射的 Gram 核，我们证明从 PU(n,1) 到无限维复双曲空间的全纯等距群的每个连续不可约表示的平移长度因子 t 与带符号 Cartan 因子 s 满足 s=±t。结合已知的端点刚性、Monod 构造和 Ruiz Stolowicz 完全不变定理，得到分类：全纯共轭类由 (t,ε)∈(0,1)×{±1} 参数化。允许反全纯共轭则识别两个符号。利用 Monod 的自动连续性论证和相同的边界刚性，我们还分类了无限维复双曲空间的全等距群的所有不可约自表示。在共轭意义下，这些正是参数 0<t≤1 的 Monod 表示。特别地，Monod 分类定理中的标准轨道假设是自动的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For n>=2, we prove a rigidity theorem for the Hermitian boundary kernels L_{t,s}(x,y)=|1-<x,y>|^t exp(is arg(1-<x,y>)), x != y, on S^{2n-1}, with L_{t,s}(x,x)=0, t>0, and s in [-1,1]. Every finite Gram matrix of L_{t,s} has positive index at most one if and only if 0<t<=1 and s=+-t. The proof combines Fourier analysis on boundary circles with restrictions to two orthogonal complex directions. The dimension threshold is sharp. By normalizing the Gram kernels of equivariant boundary maps, we show that the translation-length factor t and the signed Cartan factor s of every continuous irreducible representation from PU(n,1) to the holomorphic isometry group of infinite-dimensional complex hyperbolic space satisfy s=+-t. Combining this with the known endpoint rigidity, Monod's constructions, and Ruiz Stolowicz's complete-invariant theorem yields the classification: the holomorphic conjugacy classes are parametrized by (t,epsilon) in (0,1) x {+-1}. Allowing anti-holomorphic conjugacy identifies the two signs. Using Monod's automatic-continuity argument and the same boundary rigidity, we also classify all irreducible self-representations of the full isometry group of infinite-dimensional complex hyperbolic space. Up to conjugacy, these are precisely Monod's representations with parameter 0<t<=1. In particular, the canonical-orbit hypothesis in Monod's classification theorem is automatic.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23318
  12. [2609.23526] 关于极空间的 Moufang 条件
    On the Moufang condition for polar spaces
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究厚极空间的 Moufang 条件，构造根 elation 并给出等价定义。
    • 通过自投影表示根 elation，建立小投影群与特殊投影群的联系。
    • 证明所有有限和无限秩厚极空间都是 Moufang 的。
    • 该结果统一了 Moufang 性质的刻画，并推广到无限秩情形。
    💡 构造厚极空间中的根 elation，证明所有有限和无限秩厚极空间都是 Moufang 的。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们直接构造了嵌入在更高有限秩厚极空间中的厚广义四边形的非平凡根 elation，并证明所有这些都可以表示为长度为 4 的自投影。这明确揭示了小投影群与有限秩厚极空间的特殊投影群之间的联系。我们还证明了构造的根 elation 可扩展到环境极空间。此外，我们引入点 elation 和线 elation，并用它们给出厚极空间 Moufang 性质的等价定义，而不使用公寓的概念。这反过来允许我们定义无限秩厚极空间的 Moufang 性质。因此，我们证明所有有限和无限秩的厚极空间都是 Moufang 的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We provide direct constructions of nontrivial root elations of thick generalized quadrangles embedded in thick polar spaces of higher finite rank and show that all of these can be expressed as self-projectivities of length $4$. This reveals a connection between the little projective groups and the special projectivity groups of thick polar spaces of finite rank explicitly. We also prove that the constructed root elations extend to the ambient polar space. Furthermore, we introduce point elations and line elations and use them to give an equivalent definition of the Moufang property for thick polar spaces, without using the notion of apartments. This, in turn, allows us to define the Moufang property for thick polar spaces of infinite rank. Consequently, we show that all thick polar spaces of finite and infinite rank are Moufang.

  15. [2609.24212] Sylvester 秩完备的奇异单位群
    Exotic Unit Groups of Sylvester Rank Completions
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Sylvester 秩完备的单位群和加法群的奇异性质。
    • 证明所有强连续酉表示平凡，且 GL(Q) 是 Bourbaki 有界的。
    • 给出球覆盖的最小整数 N 的精确公式。
    • 方法上使用一致 Kazhdan 估计和秩一扰动，避免特征论方法。
    💡 证明 Sylvester 秩完备的单位群和加法群是奇异的，并给出 Bourbaki 有界性。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 R 是任意带 Sylvester 矩阵秩函数的单位环，Q 是由沿因子序列的块对角嵌入定义的矩阵系统的秩完备。我们证明 GL(Q) 和 (Q,+) 都是奇异的：每个强连续酉表示都是平凡的。我们还证明 GL(Q) 是 Bourbaki 有界的且没有非零逃逸函数。对于每个 ε>0，设 B_ε 是中心在恒等元的开球。使得 GL(Q)=B_ε^N 的最小正整数 N 是 ⌊ε^{-1}⌋+1。不需要正则性或不可约性。我们的奇异证明使用加法整数矩阵群的一致 Kazhdan 估计和秩一矩阵扰动，而不是以前使用的特征论和连续环方法。几何结论来自使用对角角子群的分解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let \(R\) be any unital ring equipped with a Sylvester matrix rank function, and let \(Q\) be the rank completion of the matrix system defined by block-diagonal embeddings along a factor sequence. We prove that both \(\GL(Q)\) and \((Q,+)\) are exotic: every strongly continuous unitary representation is trivial. We also prove that \(\GL(Q)\) is Bourbaki bounded and admits no nonzero escape function. For every \(\varepsilon>0\), let \(B_\varepsilon\) be the open ball centered at the identity. The least positive integer \(N\) such that \(\GL(Q)=B_\varepsilon^N\) is \(\lfloor\varepsilon^{-1}\rfloor+1\). Neither regularity nor irreducibility is required. Our proof of exoticness uses a uniform Kazhdan estimate for additive integer matrix groups together with rank-one matrix perturbations, rather than the character-theoretic and continuous ring methods used previously. The geometric conclusions follow from factorizations using diagonal corner subgroups.

  16. [2609.24514] 半环簇格中的连续统：区间 [V(S), V(S^0)]
    A Continuum in the Lattice of Semiring Varieties: The Interval $[\mathsf{V}(S), \mathsf{V}(S^0)]$
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 建立充分条件证明该区间具有连续统基数。
    • 应用于 S_7 和 B_2^1，解决 Jackson-Ren-Zhao 公开问题。
    • 给出非有限基性质的充分条件，并重新证明 Dolinka 定理。
    💡 证明某些半环簇区间具有连续统基数，解决 Jackson-Ren-Zhao 公开问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于加法幂等半环（ai-半环）S，设 S^0 是通过添加新元素 0 得到的 ai-半环。本文中，我们发展了一种研究 ai-半环簇区间 [V(S), V(S^0)] 的方法，该区间位于由 S 生成的簇和由 S^0 生成的簇之间。我们建立了一个一般充分条件，在该条件下该区间具有连续统的基数。这特别应用于 [V(S_7), V(S_7^0)]，其中 S_7 是一个 3 元素 ai-半环，并且是可能的最小阶的非有限基代数，从而解决了 Jackson、Ren 和 Zhao 提出的公开问题（J. Algebra 611 (2022), 211--245）。同样的结论对 [V(B_2^1), V((B_2^1)^0)] 成立，其中 B_2^1 是乘法归约为 6 元素 Brandt 半群的 ai-半环。我们还给出了 ai-半环簇中非有限基性质的充分条件。作为推论，我们获得了 Dolinka 定理（Internat. J. Algebra Comput. 17 (2007), no. 8, 1537--1551）的新证明，即 7 元素 ai-半环 (B_2^1)^0 没有有限基。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For an additively idempotent semiring (ai-semiring) $S$, let $S^0$ denote the ai-semiring obtained from $S$ by adjoining a new element $0$. In this paper, we develop an approach to investigate the interval $[\mathsf{V}(S), \mathsf{V}(S^0)]$ of ai-semiring varieties between the variety generated by $S$ and that generated by $S^0$. We establish a general sufficient condition under which this interval has the cardinality of the continuum. This is applied in particular to $[\mathsf{V}(S_7), \mathsf{V}(S_7^0)]$, where $S_7$ is a $3$-element ai-semiring and is a nonfinitely based algebra of the smallest possible order, thereby resolving an open problem proposed by Jackson, Ren, and Zhao (J. Algebra \textbf{611} (2022), 211--245). The same conclusion holds for $[\mathsf{V}(B_2^1), \mathsf{V}((B_2^1)^0)]$, where $B_2^1$ is the ai-semiring whose multiplicative reduct is the $6$-element Brandt semigroup. We also present a sufficient condition for the nonfinite basis property in ai-semiring varieties. As a corollary, we obtain a new proof of Dolinka's theorem (Internat. J. Algebra Comput. \textbf{17} (2007), no.~8, 1537--1551) that the $7$-element ai-semiring $(B_2^1)^0$ has no finite basis for its identities.

  18. [2609.24640] 逆 Baer 形变与幂零型有限单斜撑

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究具有非阿贝尔加法群的有限单斜撑的构造问题。
    • 引入逆 Baer 形变，从阿贝尔型撑产生幂零类至多二的斜撑。

    💡 引入逆 Baer 形变构造非阿贝尔幂零加法群的有限单斜撑。
    📖 中文摘要

    具有非阿贝尔加法群的有限单斜撑在很大程度上未被探索。特别是，据我们所知，以前没有构造出具有非阿贝尔幂零加法群的有限单斜撑。我们引入逆 Baer 形变，从阿贝尔型撑产生加法幂零类至多为二的斜撑。从奇数阶有限左撑 C=(A,⊕,∘) 和合适的双加性交错映射 κ:A×A→A 出发，我们定义 x+y=x⊕y⊕½κ(x,y)。得到的斜撑 IB_κ(C) 满足 Br(IB_κ(C))=C 且 [x,y]_+=κ(x,y)，并且单性从 C 传递到 IB_κ(C)。将此构造应用于由非对称乘积产生的单左撑，对于每对奇数素数 p,q 满足 p|(q-1) 和每个 m≥1，我们构造一个阶为 p^{2m(q-1)+1}q 的有限单斜撑 X_{p,q}^{(m)}，使得 (X_{p,q}^{(m)},+)≅E_{p,q}^{(m)}×C_q，其中 E_{p,q}^{(m)} 是阶为 p^{2m(q-1)+1} 且指数为 p 的 extraspecial p-群。因此，每个固定的可容许对 (p,q) 产生无限多个两两不同构的具有非阿贝尔幂零加法群（类二）的有限单斜撑。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Finite simple skew braces with non-abelian additive group remain largely unexplored. In particular, to the best of our knowledge, no finite simple skew brace with non-abelian nilpotent additive group had previously been constructed. We introduce an inverse Baer deformation which produces skew braces of additive nilpotency class at most two from braces of abelian type. Starting from a finite left brace $C=(A,\oplus,\circ)$ of odd order and a suitable biadditive alternating map $\kappa:A\times A\to A$, we define \[ x+y=x\oplus y\oplus\frac12\kappa(x,y). \] The resulting skew brace $\IB_\kappa(C)$ satisfies $\Br(\IB_\kappa(C))=C$ and $[x,y]_+=\kappa(x,y)$, and simplicity passes from $C$ to $\IB_\kappa(C)$. Applying this construction to simple left braces arising from asymmetric products, for every pair of odd primes $p,q$ with $p\mid(q-1)$ and every $m\ge1$ we construct a finite simple skew brace $X_{p,q}^{(m)}$ of order $p^{2m(q-1)+1}q$ such that \[ (X_{p,q}^{(m)},+)\cong E_{p,q}^{(m)}\times C_q, \] where $E_{p,q}^{(m)}$ is an extraspecial $p$-group of order $p^{2m(q-1)+1}$ and exponent $p$.     本文的目的是证明每个 B 型或 C 型的有限单群在其阶的每个素数 ℓ 处满足归纳块状 Alperin 权条件。对于 PSp_{2n}(q)，其中 q 是奇数且 n≥3，基于 Conlon 归纳定理的置换格论证消除了先前关于奇数非定义素数处归纳条件结果的单位三角性假设。在奇数定义特征中的 ℓ=2 处，底层根 2-子群分类中的错误和遗漏影响了任意块的权的早期参数化。我们验证了主块所需的权参数化，并使用 Jordan 约化建立所有块的归纳条件。在偶数定义特征中的奇数 ℓ 且秩至少为四时，我们使用一般权和 Jordan 约化证明归纳条件，而不假设单位三角性。对于 Ω_{2n+1}(q)，其中 q 是奇数且 n≥3，我们使用 Conlon 归纳定理消除了奇数非定义素数处的单位三角性假设。在 ℓ=2 处，我们证明了 Spin_{2n+1}(q) 的 Brauer 特征的稳定子和扩张性质，这在早期关于归纳条件的工作中被假设。我们还给出了剩余散在群 J_4、Fi'_{24}、Baby Monster 和 Monster 的归纳条件的证明，但不主张优先权。结合先前建立的情形，这些结果意味着对于每个有限群，如果其所有阶被 ℓ 整除的非阿贝尔单截断都是 B 型、C 型或散在的，则块状 Alperin 权猜想在 ℓ 处成立。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The purpose of this paper is to prove that every finite simple group of type $\mathsf B$ or $\mathsf C$ satisfies the inductive blockwise Alperin weight condition at every prime $\ell$ dividing its order. For ${\rm PSp}_{2n}(q)$, where $q$ is odd and $n\geq3$, a permutation lattice argument based on Conlon's induction theorem removes the unitriangularity assumption from earlier results on the inductive condition at odd nondefining primes. At $\ell=2$ in odd defining characteristic, errors and omissions in the underlying classification of radical $2$-subgroups affect an earlier parametrisation of weights for arbitrary blocks. We verify the required weight parametrisation for principal blocks and use Jordan reduction to establish the inductive condition for all blocks. At odd $\ell$ in even defining characteristic and rank at least four, we prove the inductive condition using generic weights and Jordan reduction, without assuming unitriangularity. For $\Omega_{2n+1}(q)$, where $q$ is odd and $n\geq3$, we remove the unitriangularity assumption at odd nondefining primes using Conlon's induction theorem. At $\ell=2$, we prove the stabiliser and extension property for Brauer characters of $\operatorname{Spin}_{2n+1}(q)$ that was assumed in earlier work on the inductive condition. We also give proofs of the inductive condition for the remaining sporadic groups $J_4$, $Fi'_{24}$, the Baby Monster and the Monster, without making a priority claim. Together with the previously established cases, these results imply that the blockwise Alperin weight conjecture holds at $\ell$ for every finite group each of whose nonabelian simple sections of order divisible by $\ell$ is of type $\mathsf B$, of type $\mathsf C$, or sporadic.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24774

    • 证明无积和序列不能有 2r-1 个中心项，排除十二非中心项层。
    • 结合相对矩准则和辛空间有限定理，并通过穷举检查验证。
    • 该结果部分推进了精确值的确定，但未完全解决。
    💡 证明 E_2×C_3^r 上无积和序列不能有 2r-1 个中心项，排除十二非中心项层。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 E_2 是阶为 3^5 且指数为三的 extraspecial 群。对于每个 r≥1，我们证明 E_2×C_3^r 上长度为 2r+11 的无积和序列不能恰好包含 2r-1 个中心项。因此，具有十二个非中心项的临界层被排除。证明结合了阿贝尔正规子群的相对矩准则和 F_3 上辛四空间中的有限定理。在显式子空间占用界下，可以实际完成为非满零和块的平衡三角形族允许每条三角形上的边权和为一。对偶循环论证将此断言约化为分支边上的势条件。所有剩余配置包含十一个最小框架之一；两个分别实现的穷举检查验证了它们的所有可容许扩展。无限制检查有 9544046 个叶子。提供了完整的源代码和执行记录。对于任意 r，d(E_2×C_3^r) 的精确值并未由该结果确定。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $E_2$ be the extraspecial group of order $3^5$ and exponent three. For every $r\ge1$, we prove that a product-one-free sequence of length $2r+11$ over $E_2\times C_3^r$ cannot contain exactly $2r-1$ central terms. Thus the critical layer with twelve noncentral terms is excluded. The proof combines a relative moment criterion for abelian normal subgroups with a finite theorem in a symplectic four-space over $\mathbb F_3$. Under explicit subspace occupancy bounds, the family of balanced triangles that can actually be completed to a nonfull zero-sum block admits edge weights summing to one on every triangle. A dual cycle argument reduces this assertion to a potential condition on branching edges. All remaining configurations contain one of eleven minimal frames; two separately implemented exhaustive checks verify all their admissible extensions. The unrestricted check has 9544046 leaves. Complete source code and execution records are supplied. The exact value of $\mathsf{d}(E_2\times C_3^r)$ for arbitrary $r$ is not determined by this result.

  26. [2609.23882] Galton-Watson 树的拟等距类
    The quasi-isometry classes of Galton--Watson trees
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Galton-Watson 树的拟等距分类。
    • 分类为射线类、全树类、链类和茂密类，取决于后代分布支撑。
    • 证明同类的树几乎必然拟等距，不同类则不是。
    • 结果应用于随机 Cantor 边界和分形渗流，并在 Lean 中形式化。
    💡 分类 Galton-Watson 树的拟等距类，并证明匹配定理。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们对于每个有限支撑后代分布，按拟等距分类 Galton-Watson 树的大尺度几何。除了平凡的有限直径情形，我们条件在无限直径上。我们发现剩余的类是射线类、全树类（包括二叉树）、对于每个可能的分支半群 Λ 的一个链类 (C_Λ) 以及茂密类。两棵独立的树在后代分布属于同一类时几乎必然允许保根的拟等距，而当属于不同类时几乎必然不是拟等距的。任何两个生存条件下的超临界实现，若具有有限支撑后代律，则几乎必然允许双向拟等距嵌入。我们证明实现的类仅取决于后代分布的支撑，而非其具体分布。对于支撑在 {1,2} 上的后代分布，我们还获得了不存在保根 D-拟等距的概率的显式指数尾界。该分类还意味着相应的随机 Cantor 边界几乎必然是拟对称等价的。条件在非灭绝上，这适用于强分离分形渗流，即使底层自相似迭代函数系统和保留参数不同。我们还分类了两个具有连续随机分支时间的树族。我们的证明使用了新的树随机图标记的自同构匹配定理，基于不匹配势的收缩估计。这些二叉树上的 Markov 标记的匹配结果可能具有独立意义。拟等距分类、可嵌入性和匹配定理在 Lean 中形式化验证。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We classify, up to quasi-isometry, the large-scale geometry of Galton--Watson trees for every finitely supported offspring distribution. Apart from the trivial finite diameter regimes, we condition on infinite diameter. We find that the remaining classes are the \emph{ray class}, the \emph{full tree class} (which includes the binary tree), one \emph{chain class} $(\mathrm{C}_\Lambda)$ for every possible branching semigroup $\Lambda$, and the \emph{bushy} class. Two independent trees almost surely admit a root-preserving quasi-isometry when their offspring distributions belong to the same class and are almost surely \textit{not} quasi-isometric when they belong to different classes. Any two survival-conditioned supercritical realisations with finitely supported offspring laws nevertheless a.s.~admit quasi-isometric embeddings in both directions. We prove that the class of a realisation depends only on the support of the offspring distribution, not its specific distribution. For offspring distributions supported on $\{1,2\}$, we additionally obtain an explicit exponential tail bound for the probability of non-existence of a root-preserving $D$-quasi-isometry. The classification also implies that the corresponding random Cantor boundaries are almost surely quasisymmetrically equivalent. Conditioned on nonextinction, this applies to strongly separated fractal percolation, even when the underlying self-similar iterated function systems and retention parameters differ. We also classify two families of trees with continuous random branching times. Our proofs use new automorphism-matching theorems for random graph labellings of trees, based on contraction estimates for mismatch potentials. These matching results for Markov labellings on the binary tree may be of independent interest. The quasi-isometry classification, embeddability, and matching theorems are formally verified in Lean.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23882

  33. [2505.21299] 确定数为2的图的代价数
    On the Cost Number of Graphs with Determining Number Two
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究图确定数与代价数之间的关系。
    • 证明确定数为2时代价数不超过4。
    • 首次解决Boutin对非平凡确定数的问题。
    • 对一般确定数n构造下界例子。
    💡 证明确定数为2的图其代价数至多为4，首次解决Boutin问题，并给出一般下界构造。
    📖 中文摘要

    图 $G$ 的区分顶点着色是使得只有恒等自同构保持该着色的顶点着色。若图允许二色区分着色，则称其为2-可区分的，其代价 $\rho(G)$ 是这种着色中最小颜色类的大小。图 $G$ 的确定数 $Det(G)$ 是最小集合 $S\subseteq V(G)$ 的大小，使得只有平凡自同构逐点固定 $S$ 的每个元素。Boutin (2013) 问 $\rho(G)$ 与 $Det(G)$ 能否任意远离。$Det(G)=1$ 情形平凡，但 $Det(G)\ge2$ 时未知。本文证明若 $Det(G)=2$ 则 $\rho(G)$ 有界且 $\rho(G)\leq4$。这是首次对非平凡固定确定数解决Boutin问题。此外，对每个固定 $Det(G)=n$，我们构造例子给出 $\rho(G)$ 上界可能的下界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A distinguishing vertex coloring of a graph $G$ is a vertex coloring such that only the identity automorphism of $G$ preserves the coloring. A graph is $2$-distinguishable if it admits a distinguishing vertex coloring with two colors, and its cost $\rho(G)$ is the minimum size of a color class in such a coloring. The determining number of a graph $G$, denoted by $Det(G)$, is the minimum size of a subset $S\subseteq V(G)$ such that only the trivial automorphism fixes every element of $S$ pointwise. Boutin (J. Combin. Math. Combin. Comput. 85: 161-171, 2013) asked if $\rho(G)$ and $Det(G)$ can be arbitrarily far apart. While the case for $Det(G) = 1$ is trivial, the answer remained unknown for $Det(G) \ge 2$. In this manuscript, we show that if $Det(G)=2$ then not only is $\rho(G)$ bounded, but in fact $\rho(G) \leq 4$. This is the first resolution of Boutin's question for any nontrivial fixed determining number. Moreover, for every fixed $Det(G)= n$, we construct examples giving a lower bound on any possible upper bound for $\rho(G)$ in terms of $n$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21299

    Coordinate recognition: General theory, Groups, and other surprises
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 证明坐标识别等价于某公式的语法性质。
    • 判定置换群、圆柱双曲群、拟单群等群类。
    💡 研究结构类的坐标识别性质，给出等价刻画及其集合论与模型论推论，并判定许多具体群类。
    📖 中文摘要

    一个结构类若其任何结构的归约积见证某种刚性现象，则称其识别坐标。我们给出该性质的若干等价刻画。该性质对归约积至少有两个显著推论，一个集合论的和一个模型论的。首先，在适当集合论假设下，与Fréchet理想相关的归约积之间的每个同构（模有限改变）提升为原结构积之间的同构。其次，附加温和假设后，它蕴含强量词消去结果。值得注意的是，我们证明一个类识别坐标当且仅当某个公式见证某种语法性质。我们还考虑许多具体结构类并判定它们是否识别坐标。我们重点讨论著名的群类，如置换群、圆柱双曲群、拟单群、自由积和图积，但也讨论其他结构类。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A class of structures \emph{recognizes coordinates} if any reduced product of structures from said class witnesses a certain kind of rigidity phenomenon. We provide several equivalent characterizations of this property. This property has (at least) two remarkable consequences, one set-theoretic and one model-theoretic, for reduced products of structures of the said class. First, under appropriate set-theoretic assumptions every isomorphism between such reduced products associated with the Fr\'echet ideal lifts (modulo a finite change) to an isomorphism between products of the original structures. Second, with an additional mild assumption, it implies a strong quantifier elimination result. Of note, we show that a class recognizes coordinates if and only if an individual formula witnesses a certain syntactic property. We also consider many concrete classes of structures and determine whether or not they recognize coordinates. We place heavy emphasis on well-known classes of groups, such as permutation groups, acylindircally hyperbolic groups, quasisimple groups, free products, and graph products, but we also discuss other classes of structures.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13673

The homology calculation uses leaf coordinates, simultaneous extensions of ordered frames, and finite-field actions on stabilizers.     📖 中文摘要

    我们证明欧氏空间的每个真子域赋予拟双曲度量后是CAT(2)。CAT性质导致Väisälä关于拟双曲测地线的唯一性、延拓、拟双曲凸性和局部测地线猜想在所有维数上得到肯定解决，推广了Herron的二维结果。截面曲率界是最优的，由至少三维中两点补集证明。我们还证明在任何拟双曲域中，半径不超过π/√2的拟双曲球面与欧氏球面是C^{1,1/2}-微分同胚的。因此，我们回答了Gehring和Vuorinen关于此类球面正则性的问题。类似技术导致Väisälä关于拟双曲球直到半径arctan(√2)/√2的欧氏凸性猜想的肯定解决。我们证明在所有维数中，拟双曲度量在凸域上是CAT(0)。这导致对Martio和Väisälä关于此类域上拟双曲球拟双曲凸性问题的肯定回答。最后，我们证明拟双曲域的可变Alexandrov曲率下界自改进为全局下界-1，且等价于域的凹性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that every proper subdomain of the Euclidean space equipped with its quasihyperbolic metric is CAT(2). The CAT property leads to a positive resolution of V\"ais\"al\"a's uniqueness, prolongation, quasihyperbolic convexity, and local geodesic conjectures on quasihyperbolic geodesics affirmatively on all dimensions, extending the two-dimensional results by Herron. The sectional curvature bound is optimal as demonstrated by the complement of two points in dimensions at least three. We also show that in any quasihyperbolic domain, quasihyperbolic spheres up to radius $\pi/\sqrt{2}$ are $\mathcal{C}^{1,\frac{1}{2}}$-diffeomorphic to the Euclidean sphere. Consequently, we answer a question by Gehring and Vuorinen on the regularity of such spheres. Similar techniques lead to a positive resolution of V\"ais\"al\"a's conjecture on the Euclidean convexity of quasihyperbolic balls up to radius $\arctan(\sqrt{2})/\sqrt{2}$. We establish that in all dimensions, the quasihyperbolic metric is CAT(0) on convex domains. This leads to a positive answer to a question by Martio and V\"ais\"al\"a on the quasihyperbolic convexity of quasihyperbolic balls on such domains. Finally, we prove that a variable Alexandrov curvature lower bound for a quasihyperbolic domain self-improves to a global lower bound of $-1$ and is equivalent to the concavity of the domain.

The $L_p$ Minkowski problem for $C$-close sets is to determine, for a real number $p$ and a nonzero finite Borel measure $\mu$ defined on $\Omega_{C^\circ}=S^{n-1}\cap \mathrm{int} C^{\circ}$, whether there exists a $C$-close set $\mathds{A}$ such that $\mu$ is the $L_p$ surface area measure of $\mathds{A}$. In this paper, we will solve the problem for $p\in (0,1)$ and for $\mu$ being a nonzero finite Borel measure on $\Omega_{C^\circ}$. Moreover, we establish the continuity of solutions to the $L_p$ Minkowski problem for $p\in [0, 1]$ in several settings.

    • 构造Hölder等价于盒距离的完备可分度量。
    💡 利用Urysohn万有空间将Gromov盒拓扑等同于弱拓扑商，并构造Hölder等价于盒距离的度量。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们通过等距嵌入 Urysohn 万有度量空间 $\mathbb U$ 研究度量测度空间的不同收敛概念。由于 $\mathbb U$ 的万有性，归一化度量测度空间的同构类集合 $\mathbb X_1$ 可典范地等同于 Borel 概率测度空间 $\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$ 的商集 $\mathscr P_\sim(\mathbb U)=\mathscr P(\mathbb U)/\sim$，其中 $\mu\sim\nu$ 若 $\nu$ 是 $\mu$ 在支撑间等距下的推前。关键利用 $\mathbb U$ 的超齐性，我们证明在上述等同下，Gromov 盒拓扑与 $\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$ 弱拓扑诱导的商拓扑一致。更定量地，$\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$ 上的截断1-Wasserstein距离诱导 $\mathbb X_1\cong\mathscr P_\sim(\mathbb U)$ 上完备可分距离 ${\sf d}_{\rm mG}$，它度量化商拓扑且Hölder等价于盒距离 $\square$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study different notions of convergence of metric measure spaces by means of isometric embeddings into the Urysohn universal metric space $\mathbb U$. Due to the universality of $\mathbb U$, the collection $\mathbb X_1$ of isomorphism classes of normalised metric measure spaces can be canonically identified with the quotient (set) $\mathscr P_\sim(\mathbb U)=\mathscr P(\mathbb U)/\sim$ of the space $\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$ of Borel probability measures on $\mathbb U$, where $\mu\sim\nu$ if $\nu$ is the pushforward of $\mu$ under an isometry between their respective supports. By making crucial use of the ultrahomogeneity of $\mathbb U$, we show that, under the above identification, Gromov's box topology on $\mathbb X_1$ coincides with the quotient topology induced by the weak topology of $\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$. More quantitatively, the truncated $1$-Wasserstein distance on $\mathscr P(\mathbb U)$ induces a complete and separable distance ${\sf d}_{\rm mG}$ on $\mathbb X_1\cong\mathscr P_\sim(\mathbb U)$, which metrises the quotient topology of $\mathscr P_\sim(\mathbb U)$ and is H\"{o}lder equivalent to the box distance $\square$.

  12. [2609.23743] 自共形系统的指数分离
    Exponential separation of self-conformal systems
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究自共形系统的指数分离条件。
    • 将Bárány等人的构造推广到任意维共形IFS。
    • 证明预Schwarzian分离蕴含强指数分离。
    • 在直线和平面得到C^2-通有性并解决维数下降猜想。
    💡 将自共形系统指数分离推广到高维，证明预Schwarzian分离蕴含强指数分离，并得到维数下降猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    在实直线上，Bárány、Kolossváry 和 Troscheit [5] 通过解析函数空间上的对偶迭代函数系统使解析自共形系统的指数分离可检验。他们的条件分离的量是预 Schwarzian 导数，这一观察让我们将构造推广到任意维共形迭代函数系统，其中预 Schwarzian 为 $T_f=\nabla\log\|Df\|$。预 Schwarzian 余循环的点态分离蕴含强指数分离条件，在一维和二维，Schwarzian 余循环的分离模 Möbius 映射蕴含相同条件。每个假设等价于一致间隙条件，且这些是 $\mathcal{C}^2$-开的。在直线上它们也是稠密的，因此两个分离条件在 $\mathcal{C}^2$-通有；这改进了 Bárány、Kolossváry 和 Troscheit 的通有定理。在平面，它们在 Jordan 域且单连通扩张域上稠密，两个分离条件也是 $\mathcal{C}^2$-通有。在至少三维，每个共形映射是 Möbius，故没有系统满足模 Möbius 映射的条件，预 Schwarzian 假设变为极点分离条件，当生成元收缩足够强且远离相似时稠密，但一般不稠密。在 Jordan 域上，平面通有性和 Feng-Rapaport [13] 的定理解决了 $\mathcal{C}^2$-通有平面单射生成元系统的维数下降猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    On the real line, B\'ar\'any, Kolossv\'ary, and Troscheit [5] made exponential separation of analytic self-conformal systems checkable through a dual iterated function system on a space of analytic functions. The quantity their condition separates is the pre-Schwarzian derivative, and this observation lets us carry the construction to conformal iterated function systems in every dimension, where the pre-Schwarzian is $T_f=\nabla\log\|Df\|$. A pointwise separation of the pre-Schwarzian cocycle implies the strong exponential separation condition, and in dimensions one and two separation of the Schwarzian cocycle implies the same condition modulo M\"obius maps. Each hypothesis is equivalent to a uniform gap condition, and these are $\mathcal{C}^2$-open. On the line they are also dense, so both separation conditions are $\mathcal{C}^2$-generic there; this sharpens the genericity theorem of B\'ar\'any, Kolossv\'ary, and Troscheit, whose open and dense set carries only the plain condition. In the plane they are dense on Jordan domains with simply connected extension domains, and both separation conditions are $\mathcal{C}^2$-generic there too. In dimensions at least three every conformal map is M\"obius, so no system satisfies the condition modulo M\"obius maps, and the pre-Schwarzian hypothesis becomes a pole-separation condition, dense when the generators contract strongly enough and stay away from the similarities, but not dense in general. On Jordan domains, the planar genericity and a theorem of Feng and Rapaport [13] settle the dimension drop conjecture for $\mathcal{C}^2$-generic planar systems with injective generators.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23743
  13. [2609.24131] 凸体的随机直径
    On random diameters of convex bodies
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究凸体随机截面的直径上下界。

    💡 研究凸体随机截面的直径，给出上下界并应用于p-椭球和信息复杂性理论。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $K \subset \mathbb{R}^N$ 是内部包含原点的凸体。本文研究 $K$ 的随机截面的直径，并根据 $K$ 的几何参数给出上下界。我们的界以大概率成立，并为这一广泛研究的主题提供新见解。上界补充了所谓的低 $M^*$-估计，在许多情况下更尖锐。我们给出的两个下界性质不同：取决于所讨论的体，每个可能更好，在许多有趣情形也与上界匹配。随后，我们将结果应用于确定 $p$-椭球的随机直径，改进已知结果并在许多情况下达到尖锐估计。一个显著应用是信息复杂性理论，我们建立了一个简单（且本质最优）的二分类，回应 Hinrichs、Prochno 和 Sonnleitner 2023 年提出的自然猜想。我们的解精确确定了何时用随机（高斯）信息替换用于从 $p$-椭球恢复向量的最优信息是有用的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $K \subset \mathbb{R}^N$ be a convex body containing the origin in its interior. In this work, we study the diameters of random sections of $K$ and derive upper and lower bounds for them in terms of geometric parameters of $K$. Our bounds hold with large probability, and they offer new insights into this widely studied subject. Our upper bound complements the so-called low $M^*$-estimate and in many cases it is much sharper. The two lower bounds that we give are each of a different nature: depending on the body in question each time, either could be better, and in many interesting cases it matches the upper bound too. Subsequently, we apply our results to determine random diameters of $p$-ellipsoids (images of $\ell_p$ balls under diagonal operators), improving upon previously known results and achieving sharp estimates in many cases. One notable application is to Information-Based Complexity Theory, where we manage to establish a simple (and essentially optimal) dichotomy in response to a very natural conjecture posed by Hinrichs, Prochno and Sonnleitner in 2023. Our solution settles precisely when it is useful to replace the optimal information used for the recovery of vectors from a $p$-ellipsoid with random (Gaussian) information, which can be more practical to obtain.

    • 通过将方向集提升到圆的全覆盖并构造松弛控制问题，利用计算机辅助验证存储函数。

    • 该结果解决了长期猜想，并展示了计算机辅助证明在几何优化中的潜力。
    💡 论文用计算机辅助证明了对数螺线在未知直线搜索问题中具有最优竞争比，解决了Baeza-Yates等人的猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    一艘船从平面上的某点出发以单位速度移动，必须到达一条未知的直线，该直线的距离和方向均未知。路径的竞争比定义为到达直线的时间除以其距离在所有直线上的上确界。Baeza-Yates、Culberson和Rawlins猜想对数螺线（比率 C_sp = 13.8111351794611...）是最优的。我们给出了计算机辅助证明。路径是任意的：到起点的距离和极角都可以减小。证明将找到的方向集提升到圆的全覆盖上，其中展开极角只会增加它（直线上的Kneser-Poulsen）；然后一个带有单调最终源的记账不等式将覆盖的测度限制在一个三状态松弛控制问题的回报上，其中向内运动是普通控制，低于保证圆盘的偏移是脉冲。一个显式的 C^1 存储函数（张量三次B样条加上闭式项）在该问题的耗散不等式下满足螺线的水平，并且仅在螺线处取等号；这通过约10^6个Arb球算术盒子、一个精确射流和螺线处的区间Hessian验证。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A ship starts at a point of the plane and moves at unit speed; it has to reach an unknown straight line, of which neither the distance nor the direction is known. The competitive ratio of a path is the supremum, over all lines, of the time at which the line is reached divided by its distance. Baeza-Yates, Culberson and Rawlins conjectured that a logarithmic spiral, with ratio $C_{\mathrm{sp}} = 13.8111351794611\ldots$, is optimal. We give a computer-assisted proof. Paths are arbitrary: the distance from the start and the polar angle may both decrease. The proof lifts the set of found directions to the universal cover of the circle, where unfolding the polar angle can only increase it (Kneser-Poulsen on the line); a bookkeeping inequality with a monotone final source then bounds the covered measure by the reward of a three-state relaxed control problem, in which inward motion is an ordinary control and excursions below the guaranteed disk are impulses. An explicit $C^1$ storage function, a tensor cubic B-spline plus a closed-form term, satisfies the dissipation inequalities of that problem at the spiral's level and is tight only at the spiral; this is verified with about $10^6$ boxes of Arb ball arithmetic, an exact jet and an interval Hessian at the spiral.
