  16. [2609.24623] 从维数下降到非周期序
    From Dimension Drop to Aperiodic Order
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究参数族自相似集的拓扑分类及其内部性二分法。
    • 综合自相似集、成就集和投影集三种视角，得到完整拓扑分类并坍缩为二分类。
    • 发现二分类可视化产生非周期平铺，且无平移对称性。
    • 该平铺描述了维数下降现象，并具有替换结构。
    💡 论文完整分类了一类自相似集的拓扑结构，并发现其内部性分类产生一个非周期平铺，揭示了维数下降现象。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们考虑参数族集合 E(x,y) = {∑_{n=1}^∞ ε_n/4^n : (ε_n) ∈ {0,x,y,x+y}^N}，其中 (x,y) ∈ N^2。该族可以从三个视角看待：(a) 作为齐次自相似集；(b) 作为双几何级数的成就集；(c) 作为四角Cantor集的‘有理’正交投影集。我们综合这三种视角，对 (x,y) ∈ N^2 给出了 E(x,y) 的完整拓扑分类。接着，我们根据 E(x,y) 是否有内部将这个拓扑分类坍缩为一个二分类。当这个二分类被可视化时，它揭示了格 N^2 的一个双色平铺 T，尽管看起来有结构，但结果是非周期的；事实上，我们证明它没有非平凡平移对称性。由于模型的刚性，这个二分类同时捕捉了几个二分法。最显著的是，当族 {E(x,y)} 通过自相似集理论观察时，T 可以看作描述了族内维数下降的出现。最后我们检查平铺非周期序的机制。通过考虑平铺的数论性质，我们刻画了其替换结构，并发现 T 是四个‘隐藏’算术定义状态上的替换平铺的一个因子。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We consider the parametrised family of sets $$ E(x,y)=\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{\varepsilon_n}{4^n}: (\varepsilon_n) \in \{0,x,y,x+y\}^{\mathbb{N}}\right\} $$ for $(x,y) \in \mathbb{N}^2$. This family can be viewed through three lenses: (a) as homogeneous self-similar sets; (b) as achievement sets of bi-geometric series; or (c) as the set of `rational' orthogonal projections of the four-corner Cantor set. We synthesise these three perspectives to obtain a complete topological classification of $E(x,y)$ for $(x,y) \in \mathbb{N}^2$. Next, we collapse this topological classification to a binary one according to whether or not $E(x,y)$ has interior. When this binary classification is visualised, it reveals a two-colour tiling $T$ of the lattice $\mathbb N^2$, which, despite being visibly structured, turns out to be aperiodic; indeed, we prove it has no non-trivial translational symmetries. Due to the rigidity of our model, this same binary classification simultaneously captures several dichotomies. Most notably, when the family $\{E(x,y)\}_{(x,y) \in \mathbb{N}^2}$ is viewed through the theory of self-similar sets, $T$ can be seen to describe the emergence of dimension drop within the family. Finally we examine the mechanism underlying the tiling's aperiodic order. By considering the number-theoretic properties of the tiling, we characterise its substitution structure, and discover that $T$ is a factor of a substitution tiling on four ``hidden'' arithmetically defined states.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24623

    📖 中文摘要

    目前仍未知是否每个棱柱体都有非重叠边展开，这是长期未解决的‘Dürer问题’的一个特例。最近，嵌套棱柱体已被解决[Rad24]，通过混合（在某种意义下）两种自然展开：花瓣展开和带状展开。带状展开因一个特定反例而失败[O'R13b]。本文的主要贡献是刻画了嵌套棱柱体的带状展开何时确实导致非重叠展开。特别地，我们表明所提到的反例在某种意义上是唯一可能的反例。尽管这一结果没有扩大已知具有边展开的形状类，但其证明在多个方面扩展了我们的理解，发展了可能有助于解决非嵌套情形的工具。

    🌐 English Abstract

    It remains unknown if every prismatoid has a nonoverlapping edge-unfolding, a special case of the long-unsolved "D\"urer's problem." Recently nested prismatoids have been settled [Rad24] by mixing (in some sense) the two natural unfoldings, petal-unfolding and band-unfolding. Band-unfolding fails due to a specific counterexample [O'R13b]. The main contribution of this paper is a characterization when a band-unfolding of a nested prismatoid does in fact result in a nonoverlapping unfolding. In particular, we show that the mentioned counterexample is in a sense the only possible counterexample. Although this result does not expand the class of shapes known to have an edge-unfolding, its proof expands our understanding in several ways, developing tools that may help resolve the non-nested case.

  19. [2606.17051] 平面中Gromov-Hausdorff距离的常数因子近似
    A constant-factor approximation of the Gromov-Hausdorff distance in the plane
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究平面有限点集间Gromov-Hausdorff距离的近似计算。
    • 提出局部算法，通过猜测直径对和窗口匹配，结合2-SAT选择反射符号。
    • 得到多项式时间常数因子近似，失真为 O(d_GH)。
    • 该结果首次在平面实现常数因子近似，并留下双射情形的开放问题。
    💡 论文给出了平面有限点集间Gromov-Hausdorff距离的首个多项式时间常数因子近似算法。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出了欧几里得平面中有限点集之间Gromov-Hausdorff距离 d_GH 的首个多项式时间常数因子近似；在固定欧几里得维数中，此前仅在一维直线上已知这样的近似（Majhi, Vitter和Wenk, 2024）。全局对齐无法成功：经典的维数下降击败了等距对齐，多重性间隙击败了双射对齐，反射障碍击败了任何单一全局反射模式下的排序。因此算法是局部的。猜测一个直径对的像将所有点的纵向坐标固定在 O(d_GH) 内。高度在窗口中读取，其高度跨度至多是其长度的固定倍数，其中链式论证使每个兼容匹配在平面中局部化。然后通过2-SAT为每个窗口选择一个反射符号；在正确的框架和猜测下，任何解都给出失真为 O(d_GH) 的对应。对于 d_GH 的双射相对物（双射上最小加性失真的一半），同样的方案将平面问题简化为一个匹配问题，我们将其留作开放。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We give the first polynomial-time constant-factor approximation of the Gromov-Hausdorff distance d_GH between finite point sets in the Euclidean plane; in fixed Euclidean dimension such an approximation was previously known only on the line (Majhi, Vitter and Wenk, 2024). Global alignment cannot succeed: the classical dimension drop defeats alignment by isometries, a multiplicity gap defeats alignment by bijections, and a reflection barrier defeats sorting under any single global reflection pattern. The algorithm is therefore local. Guessing the images of one diameter pair pins every point's longitudinal coordinate to within O(d_GH). Heights are read in windows whose height spread is at most a fixed multiple of their length, where a chain argument makes every compatible match local in the plane. One reflection sign per window is then chosen by 2-SAT; at the right frame and guess, any solution yields a correspondence of distortion O(d_GH). For the bijective relative of d_GH, half the least additive distortion over bijections, the same scheme reduces the planar problem to a matching question that we leave open.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17051

  1. [2609.22297] 类域塔的 Iwasawa 理论
    Iwasawa theory of class field towers
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究类域塔中伽罗瓦群的滤链渐近性质。
    • 假设 μ-不变量为正，证明生成元数 d_n 的渐近公式。
    • 证明增广商与自由 pro-p 群的首项渐近相同。
    • 得到 Hilbert 级数、指数增长率和 Zassenhaus 商的渐近。
    💡 研究类域塔中伽罗瓦群的增广滤链和 Zassenhaus 滤链的渐近行为。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 p 是奇素数，K_∞/K 是 ℤ_p-扩张。对于每个有限层 K_n，设 G_n 是其最大非分歧 pro-p 扩张的伽罗瓦群。我们研究当 n 趋于无穷时群 G_n 的增广滤链和 Zassenhaus 滤链。假设经典 Iwasawa μ-不变量为正。如果 d_n 表示 G_n 的最小生成元数，则存在整数 δ>0 使得 d_n=δ p^n+O(1)，而最小关系数是 O(p^n)。对于每个固定的 m≥2，当 n→∞ 时，我们证明第 m 个增广商与 d_n 个生成元的自由 pro-p 群具有相同的首项渐近。我们还获得了 Hilbert 级数、增广滤链的指数增长率和 Zassenhaus 商的相应渐近。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $K_\infty/K$ be a $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extension. For each finite layer $K_n$, let $G_n$ be the Galois group of its maximal unramified pro-$p$ extension. We study the augmentation and Zassenhaus filtrations of the groups $G_n$ as $n$ tends to infinity. Assume that the classical Iwasawa $\mu$-invariant is positive. If $d_n$ denotes the minimal number of generators of $G_n$, then there is an integer $\delta>0$ such that $d_n=\delta p^n+O(1)$, while the minimal number of relations is $O(p^n)$. For each fixed $m\geq2$, as $n\to\infty$, we show that the $m$-th augmentation quotient has the same leading asymptotic as that of a free pro-$p$ group on $d_n$ generators. We also obtain corresponding asymptotics for the Hilbert series, the exponential growth rate of the augmentation filtration, and the Zassenhaus quotients.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22297

This places multiplication in a sharply different category from powers, composition, and other algebraic operations for which reverse-sparsity principles are known. It also gives an unconditional, degree-free obstruction to sparse factorization, complementing positive factor-sparsity results that impose hypotheses on coefficient height, exponent positions, reciprocal structure, or degree bounds.

  4. [2609.22340] 两个分拆函数模3的二次剩余二分法
    A quadratic-residue dichotomy for two partition functions modulo 3
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究两个分拆函数模3的同余性质。
    • 通过生成函数模3约化和二次型分割，证明同余保持或交换取决于5的二次剩余性。
    • 得到显式同余式及自相似同余族。
    • 该结果揭示了分拆函数与二次互反律的深刻联系。
    💡 论文证明了两个分拆函数模3的 congruences，其保持或交换取决于5是否为模p的二次剩余。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 f_{0,1,4}(n) 表示将 n 分拆成模5余0、1或4的部分且每部分最多使用两次的分拆数，f_{0,2,3}(n) 类似地定义。我们证明对于每个素数 p ≡ 3 (mod 4)，存在显式非负整数 a(p) 和 b(p)，由 20a(p) ≡ -9 和 20b(p) ≡ -1 (mod p^2) 确定，使得当 p ≡ ±1 (mod 5) 时，f_{0,1,4}(p^2 m + a(p)) 和 f_{0,2,3}(p^2 m + b(p)) 模3分别同余于 f_{0,1,4}(m) 和 f_{0,2,3}(m)，而当 p ≡ ±2 (mod 5) 时交换。由二次互反律，这两个函数在5是模p的二次剩余时保持，否则交换。最小情形是 f_{0,1,4}(9m) ≡ f_{0,2,3}(m) 和 f_{0,2,3}(9m+4) ≡ f_{0,1,4}(m)。证明通过 (1-x)^ℓ ≡ 1-x^ℓ (mod ℓ) 和 Jacobi 三重积将每个生成函数模3约化为 Rogers-Ramanujan 型 theta 函数的平方，然后利用 -1 是模p的二次非剩余对所得二元二次型进行分割。作为推论，我们对每个这样的 p 得到模 p 的例外同余，以及模 p^{dn}（d=2或4）的自相似同余族，常数为 9(p^{dn}-1)/20 和 (p^{dn}-1)/20。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $f_{0,1,4}(n)$ denote the number of partitions of $n$ into parts congruent to 0, 1 or 4 modulo 5 with each part used at most twice, and let $f_{0,2,3}(n)$ be defined analogously for parts congruent to 0, 2 or 3 modulo 5. We prove that for every prime $p \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ there are explicit non-negative integers $a(p)$ and $b(p)$, determined by $20a(p) \equiv -9$ and $20b(p) \equiv -1 \pmod{p^2}$, such that $f_{0,1,4}(p^2m + a(p))$ and $f_{0,2,3}(p^2m + b(p))$ are congruent modulo 3 to $f_{0,1,4}(m)$ and $f_{0,2,3}(m)$ when $p \equiv \pm 1 \pmod 5$, and to $f_{0,2,3}(m)$ and $f_{0,1,4}(m)$ when $p \equiv \pm 2 \pmod 5$. By quadratic reciprocity the two functions are preserved exactly when 5 is a quadratic residue modulo $p$, and interchanged otherwise. The smallest cases are $f_{0,1,4}(9m) \equiv f_{0,2,3}(m)$ and $f_{0,2,3}(9m+4) \equiv f_{0,1,4}(m)$. The proof reduces each generating function modulo 3 to the square of a Rogers-Ramanujan-type theta function by means of $(1-x)^{\ell} \equiv 1-x^{\ell} \pmod{\ell}$ and the Jacobi triple product, and then dissects the resulting binary quadratic form using the fact that $-1$ is a quadratic non-residue modulo $p$. As corollaries we obtain, for each such $p$, a congruence with exceptions on a progression of modulus $p$, and an infinite family of self-similarity congruences on progressions of modulus $p^{dn}$ with $d = 2$ or $d = 4$ and constants $9(p^{dn}-1)/20$ and $(p^{dn}-1)/20$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22340

    • 给出判别式公式、不可约性条件和单基因性准则。
    • 迭代变换可构造任意2^k n次单基因域，并显式构造512次域。
    • 该结果为密码学提供了大次数单基因域的构造方法。
    💡 论文通过回文变换构造了单基因数域，并给出了判别式公式和不可约性条件，迭代可得到任意2^k n次单基因域。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于次数为 n 的首一不可约多项式 f ∈ Z[x] 和整数 c，我们研究回文变换 F(x) = x^n f(x + x^{-1} + c)，它产生一个次数为 2n 的多项式。我们给出了判别式公式 disc(F) = f(c+2)f(c-2)disc(f)^2，F 不可约的充分条件，以及一个准则表明当 f 是单基因的且 f(c+2)f(c-2) 无平方因子时 F 是单基因的，并给出了匹配的非单基因准则。迭代该变换从固定基多项式产生次数为 2^k n 的单基因数域。作为一个显式例子，我们从 x^4 + x + 1 构造了一个次数为512的单基因域。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a monic irreducible $f \in \mathbb{Z}[x]$ of degree $n$ and an integer $c$, we study the palindromic transform $F(x) = x^n f(x + x^{-1} + c)$, which produces a polynomial of degree $2n$. We give a discriminant formula $\operatorname{disc}(F) = f(c+2)f(c-2)\operatorname{disc}(f)^2$, sufficient conditions for the irreducibility of $F$, and a criterion showing that $F$ is monogenic when $f$ is monogenic and $f(c+2)f(c-2)$ is squarefree, together with a matching non-monogenicity criterion. Iterating the transform yields monogenic number fields of degree $2^k n$ from a fixed base polynomial. As an explicit example, we construct a monogenic field of degree 512 from $x^4 + x + 1$.

    • 对初等阿贝尔p-群得到尖锐估计τ_p(E_m) ≍ m^2。

    💡 论文改进了Ozaki定理，利用Frattini结构将实现给定p-群的数域次数从|G|量级降低到O_p(n^2)。
    📖 中文摘要

    Ozaki证明了每个有限p-群都可作为某数域的最大非分歧p-扩张的Galois群。Hajir、Maire和Ramakrishna使该定理有效，得到基域次数为 |G| 量级。在本文中，我们通过考虑 G 的Frattini结构来降低该次数。更精确地，对于奇素数 p 和阶为 p^n 的有限p-群 G，我们证明 τ_p(G) ≤ p^{ℓ_Φ(G) + ⌈log_p( (n+2 choose 2) + 1 )⌉}。这里 τ_p(G) 是实现 G 为其p-类塔群的最小数域次数，ℓ_Φ(G) 是 G 的迭代Frattini长度。因此，对于有界Frattini长度的群，阶量级界 p^n 被二次界 O_p(n^2) 取代。对于 E_m = (Z/pZ)^m，我们进一步证明尖锐估计 τ_p(E_m) ≍_p m^2。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Ozaki proved that every finite $p$-group occurs as the Galois group of a maximal unramified $p$-extension of a number field. Hajir, Maire and Ramakrishna made this theorem effective, obtaining a base-field degree of order $|G|$. In this article, we reduce that degree by taking the Frattini structure of $G$ into account. More precisely, for an odd prime $p$ and a finite $p$-group $G$ of order $p^n$, we prove \[ \tau_p(G) \leq p^{\ell_\Phi(G)+ \left\lceil\log_p\left(\binom{n+2}{2}+1\right)\right\rceil}. \] Here $\tau_p(G)$ is the least degree of a number field realizing $G$ as its $p$-class tower group, and $\ell_\Phi(G)$ is the iterated Frattini length of $G$. Thus, for groups of bounded Frattini length, the order-scale bound $p^n$ is replaced by the quadratic bound $O_p(n^2)$. For $E_m=(\Z/p\Z)^m$, we further prove the sharp estimate $\tau_p(E_m)\asymp_p m^2$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22766

    记 ω(n) 为自然数 n 的不同素因子个数。2007年，Granville和Soundararajan给出了一种相当新的方法来计算高阶矩 ∑_{n≤x}(ω(n)-log log x)^k，对于广泛的整数 k≥2。在本注记中，我们将该方法应用于 ω_z(n)，它表示 n 的不超过 z 的不同素因子个数。特别地，对于奇数 k≥3，我们在对 k、z 和 x 的某些限制下，导出了 ∑_{n≤x}(ω_z(n)-log log z)^k 的渐近公式。此外，我们提及了该方法的框架。

In this notes, we shall apply the method for $\omega_{z}(n)$ which denotes the number of distinct prime divisors $\leq z$ of $n$. Especially, for odd integers $k\geq 3$, we lead asymptotic formulas for $\sum_{n\leq x}(\omega_{z}(n)-\log\log z)^{k}$, under certain restrictions on $k$, $z$, and $x$. Also, we refer to a framework of the approach.

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $m\geq 1$ be a fixed integer, $a$ an integer satisfying $(a,m)=1$, and $z\geq 1$ a real parameter. Denote by $\omega_{z}(n;m,a)$ the number of distinct prime divisors $p$ of $n$ satisfying $p\equiv a\, (m)$ and $p\leq z$. We study an asymptotic behaviour of $\sum_{n\leq x}\left(\omega_{z}(n;m,a)-\frac{1}{\varphi(m)}\log\log z\right)^{k}$ as $x\to\infty$ for a wide range of positive integer $k\geq 2$, where $\varphi(\cdot)$ is the Euler function. Following a method of Granville and Soundararajan we lead an asymptotic formula for the above. Also, we investigate $\sum_{n\leq x}\left(\omega(n;m,a)-\frac{1}{\varphi(m)}\log\log x\right)^{k}$, where $\omega(n;m,a)$ denotes the number of distinct prime divisors $p$ of $n$ such that $p\equiv a\, (m)$.

    Romanoff type theorems for polynomials over finite fields
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限域上多项式表示为不可约多项式加幂和的问题。
    • 证明某些多项式有至少c log n个表示，正比例多项式有至少两个表示。
    • 对于幂和，证明正比例可表示当∑ r_i^{-1} ≥ 1。
    • 该结果推广了Romanoff定理到有限域多项式情形。
    💡 论文研究了有限域上多项式表示为不可约多项式加幂和的问题，证明了正比例多项式有多个表示。
    📖 中文摘要

    给定有限域上次数为 δ>0 的多项式 g，我们研究次数为 n 的首一多项式 f，它可以写成 f = h + g^k，其中 h 是次数为 n 的首一不可约多项式，k∈N 且 δk<n。遵循Erdős，我们证明某些 f 至少有 c log n 个这样的表示。如果 δρ_g<1，其中 ρ_g = φ_q(g)/|g|，则正比例的次数为 n 的多项式至少有两次这样的表示。更一般地，对于幂和 g^{⌊k_i^{r_i}⌋}，我们证明当 ∑_{i=1}^t r_i^{-1} ≥ 1 时，正比例的次数为 n 的多项式可表示，而某些模某个不可约多项式的剩余类不包含可表示多项式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Given a polynomial \(g\) of degree \(\delta>0\) over a finite field, we study monic polynomials \(f\) of degree \(n\) that can be written as \(f=h+g^k\), where \(h\) is a monic irreducible polynomial of degree \(n\) and \(k\in\mathbb{N}\) with \(\delta k<n\). Following Erd\H{o}s, we show that some \(f\) admit at least \(c\log n\) such representations. If \(\delta\rho_g<1\), where \(\rho_g=\varphi_q(g)/|g|\), then a positive proportion of degree-\(n\) polynomials admits at least two such representations. More generally, for sums of powers \(g^{\lfloor k_i^{r_i}\rfloor}\), we prove that a positive proportion of polynomials of degree \(n\) is representable whenever \(\sum_{i=1}^{t}r_i^{-1}\ge 1\), while certain residue classes modulo some irreducible polynomial contain no representable polynomial.

    📖 中文摘要

    设 n≥1，ℓ 为奇素数。设 G = PGSp_{2n}(F_ℓ) 或 GSp_{2n}(F_ℓ)，并固定一个忠实传递置换表示 π: G → S_d。我们研究次数为 d 的数域，其Galois闭包的Galois群为 G，且关联的置换表示为 π。对于 σ∈S_d，记 ind(σ) 为其置换指数，即 ind(σ) = d - #{σ在{1,...,d}上的轨道数}。如果 τ 表示辛转置或其在射影辛群中的像，我们证明这样的域其绝对判别式至多 X 的数量下界为常数倍 X^{1/(2n ind(π(τ)))}。对于自然向量和射影作用，我们得到的指数在 ℓ→∞ 时渐近为Malle猜想弱形式预测的 1/(4n)。这些域由一参数超椭圆曲线族的Jacobian上的模 ℓ Galois表示构造。证明结合了该族的大辛单值群和无平方筛法。

    🌐 English Abstract

For $\sigma\in S_d$, write $\operatorname{ind}(\sigma)$ for its permutation index, namely $\operatorname{ind}(\sigma) = d-\#\{\text{orbits of $\sigma$ on $\{1,\ldots,d\}$}\}$. The fields are constructed from the mod-$\ell$ Galois representations attached to the Jacobians of a one-parameter family of hyperelliptic curves. The proof combines large symplectic monodromy for this family with a squarefree sieve.

We give a complete classification of the pairs for which the mixed restricted sumset $A\mathbin{\widehat+} B=\{x+y:x\in A,\ y\in B,\ x\ne y\}$ has size $\min\{p,|A|+|B|-2\}$. The main new contribution concerns the interior range $|A|+|B|\le p-1$. For $|B|\ge3$ and $|A|-|B|\ge3$, criticality forces $A$ and $B$ to be common-endpoint arithmetic progressions, apart from a single common affine orbit with $(p,|A|,|B|)=(13,7,4)$. For $|A|-|B|\in\{1,2\}$, criticality itself forces $B\subsetneq A$, after which the restricted self-sum determines the deletion patterns. Together with the remaining cases, this yields the complete classification.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Apéry 型序列的两步超同余猜想。
    • 通过超几何函数和差分算子方法，构造解析商并计算特征多项式。
    • 证明更强的赋值估计，并处理例外素数。
    • 结果验证了 Sun 的猜想，并展示了有限计算与符号证明的结合。
    💡 证明了 Zhi-Hong Sun 关于 Apéry 型序列的两步超同余猜想，并给出更强的赋值估计。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $G_n=\sum_{k=0}^n4^k\binom{2n-2k}{n-k}^{2}\binom{2k}{k}$。Zhi-Hong Sun 猜想：对于素数 $p\equiv3\pmod4$，正奇数 $m$ 和 $r\ge2$，两步同余 $G_{(mp^r-1)/2}\equiv p^2G_{(mp^{r-2}-1)/2}\pmod {p^{2r-1}}$ 成立。我们证明更强的赋值陈述 $G_{(p^2M-1)/2}-p^2G_{(M-1)/2}\in p^{2v_p(M)+3}\mathbb Z_p$ 对所有正奇数 $M$ 成立。证明将和式转化为终止的 ${}_3F_2$，构造消去数字转移算子，并识别其三次差分算子的二维解析商。商算子的特征多项式为 $X^2-p^2$；其第二迹通过 Gross-Koblitz 公式和 Greene 的有限域 Dixon 恒等式求值。对数损失 Green 逆将此谱恒等式转化为实际的积分解析原函数。例外素数 $3$ 需要有限精确的 PARI/GP 证书，而无限尾部用符号界估计。因此计算是有限、可重复的，并与证明的一致部分分离。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $G_n=\sum_{k=0}^n4^k\binom{2n-2k}{n-k}^{2}\binom{2k}{k}$. Zhi-Hong Sun conjectured that, for primes $p\equiv3\pmod4$, positive odd integers $m$, and $r\ge2$, the two-step congruence $G_{(mp^r-1)/2}\equiv p^2G_{(mp^{r-2}-1)/2}\pmod {p^{2r-1}}$ holds. We prove the stronger valuation statement $G_{(p^2M-1)/2}-p^2G_{(M-1)/2}\in p^{2v_p(M)+3}\mathbb Z_p$ for every positive odd $M$. The proof converts the sum to a terminating ${}_3F_2$, constructs a cancelled digit-transfer operator, and identifies a two-dimensional analytic quotient of its cubic difference operator. The quotient operator has characteristic polynomial $X^2-p^2$; its second trace is evaluated through the Gross--Koblitz formula and Greene's finite-field Dixon identity. A logarithmic-loss Green inverse converts this spectral identity into an actual integral analytic primitive. The exceptional prime $3$ requires a finite exact PARI/GP certificate, while the infinite tail is bounded symbolically. Thus the computation is finite, reproducible, and separated from the uniform part of the proof.

    Lower-order terms in mean-square formulas for the Euler--Zagier double zeta-function and regularized multiple zeta values
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 Euler-Zagier 双 zeta 函数均方的低阶项。
    • 通过解析方法改进已有结果，得到余项 $o(T)$ 的渐近公式。
    • 发现额外振荡项，并将常数与多重 zeta 值联系。
    • 深化了对双 zeta 函数均方行为的理解。
    💡 改进了 Euler-Zagier 双 zeta 函数均方公式，得到余项 $o(T)$ 的渐近公式，并联系多重 zeta 值。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究当第二个变量垂直移动时 Euler-Zagier 双 zeta 函数的均方。Matsumoto 和 Tsumura 以及 Ikeda、Matsuoka 和 Nagata 先前获得了绝对收敛区域内外的均方公式。特别地，后者确定了以下三种边界情况的主项：\[ \zeta_2\left(1+a,\frac12+it\right),\qquad \zeta_2\left(1+ib,\frac12+it\right),\qquad \zeta_2\left(1-a,\frac12+a+it\right), \] 其中 $a\in\mathbb C$ 且 $\Re a>0$，$b\in\mathbb R$ 固定。第一和第三种情况以及第二种情况当 $b\neq0$ 时的均方主项阶为 $T\log T$。当 $b=0$ 时，第二种情况退化为角点 $(1,1/2)$，主项阶为 $T(\log T)^3$。在本文中，我们通过获得余项为 $o(T)$ 的渐近公式来改进这些结果。在 $b\neq0$ 的情况下，出现额外的 $T$ 阶振荡项。我们还证明，在参数点 $(s_1,\sigma_2)=(k,\ell/2)$（整数 $k\geq1$，$\ell\geq2$）处，均方公式中乘以 $T$ 的常数是多重 zeta 值的线性组合。在边界点 $(k,1/2)$（整数 $k\geq1$）处，相应发散和的和有限部分用调和正则化多重 zeta 值表示。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the mean square of the Euler--Zagier double zeta-function when the second variable moves vertically. Mean-square formulas in and beyond the region of absolute convergence were previously obtained by Matsumoto and Tsumura and by Ikeda, Matsuoka and Nagata. In particular, the latter authors determined the leading terms in the following three boundary cases: \[ \zeta_2\left(1+a,\frac12+it\right),\qquad \zeta_2\left(1+ib,\frac12+it\right),\qquad \zeta_2\left(1-a,\frac12+a+it\right), \] where $a\in\mathbb C$ with $\Re a>0$ and $b\in\mathbb R$ are fixed. The mean squares in the first and third cases, as well as in the second case when $b\neq0$, have leading terms of order $T\log T$. When $b=0$, the second case reduces to the corner $(1,1/2)$, where the leading term is of order $T(\log T)^3$. In the present paper, we refine these results by obtaining asymptotic formulas with remainder $o(T)$. In the case $b\neq0$, an additional oscillatory term of order $T$ occurs. We also show that, at the parameter points $(s_1,\sigma_2)=(k,\ell/2)$ with integers $k\geq1$ and $\ell\geq2$, the constant multiplying $T$ in the mean-square formula is a linear combination of multiple zeta values. At the boundary points $(k,1/2)$ with integers $k\geq1$, the harmonic finite parts of the corresponding divergent sums are expressed in terms of harmonic regularized multiple zeta values.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究完全实域上椭圆曲线 CM 扭的 Selmer 群。

    💡 在完全实域上证明存在许多二次扭使 Selmer 群平凡，从而秩为零且 Tate-Shafarevich 群平凡。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 $F$ 是次数 $g\leq5$ 的完全实 Galois 数域，$\ell$ 为奇素数。设 $E/F$ 是具有 $F$-有理 $\ell$ 阶点的椭圆曲线。在显式算术和局部假设以及模 $\ell$ 的显式非消失条件下，我们证明存在 $\gg_{F,E,\ell}\frac{X^{1/(2g)}}{\log X}$ 个完全负平方类 $d\in F^\times/(F^\times)^2$，满足 $\left|\mathrm{N}_{F/\mathbb{Q}}\bigl(D(F(\sqrt{d})/F)\bigr)\right|<X $，使得 $\operatorname{Sel}_\ell(E^d,F)$ 平凡。这样的扭秩为零且 Shafarevich-Tate 群的 $\ell$-部分平凡。条件可通过有限计算验证，我们在一个例子中执行。我们将 James 和 Ono 的方法从 $\mathbb{Q}$ 提升到完全实设定，结合 Morrow 的定理（将此类扭的 Selmer 群与 CM 扩张 $F(\sqrt{d})$ 的类群联系）和 Takai 关于相对类数的不可分性定理。Takai 仅用二次 Hecke 特征扭，而 Morrow 的条件是局部的；我们将扭论证推广到本原二次剩余类特征，使两个结果兼容。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $F$ be a totally real Galois number field of degree $g\leq5$ and let $\ell$ be an odd prime. Let $E/F$ be an elliptic curve with an $F$-rational point of order $\ell$. Under explicit arithmetic and local hypotheses, together with an explicit non-vanishing condition modulo $\ell$, we prove that there are $\gg_{F,E,\ell}\frac{X^{1/(2g)}}{\log X}$ totally negative square classes $d\in F^\times/(F^\times)^2$ with $\left|\mathrm{N}_{F/\mathbb{Q}}\bigl(D(F(\sqrt{d})/F)\bigr)\right|<X $ for which $\operatorname{Sel}_\ell(E^d,F)$ is trivial. Such twists have rank zero and trivial $\ell$-part of the Shafarevich--Tate group. The condition is verifiable by a finite computation, which we carry out in an example. We lift the method of James and Ono from $\mathbb{Q}$ to the totally real setting, combining a theorem of Morrow, which relates the Selmer group of such a twist to the class group of the CM extension $F(\sqrt{d})$, with an indivisibility theorem of Takai for relative class numbers. Takai twists only by quadratic Hecke characters, whereas Morrow's conditions are local; we extend the twisting argument to primitive quadratic residue-class characters to make the two results compatible.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究主极化超特殊阿贝尔曲面同源图中分裂曲面的可检测性，这一问题与维度 $2$ 的基于同源的密码学的安全性分析相关。我们的方法使用精化 Humbert 不变量，将显式同源计算替换为五元正定二次型的本原表示问题。我们开发算法来检测 $(N,N)$-分裂并计算超特殊 Jacobian 的最小 $(N,N)$-分裂水平，而无需构造相应的同源路径。相同框架通过实二次序的判别式的本原表示检测实乘（RM）序的嵌入。对于 RM，我们使用穷举小素数数据以及对于 $227\leq p\leq1619$ 每个素数 $100{,}000$ 个随机极化，测试无平方因子判别式 $D\leq100$ 的本原表示；第一个非平凡本原表示的判别式通常很小。我们在两种情形下实验应用这些方法。对于 $11\leq p\leq251$，其中不可约主极化已知穷举，从精化 Humbert 不变量恢复的自同构数据重现了 Ibukiyama-Katsura-Oort 对不可约极化的计数。对于大参数，我们达到 $1000$ 比特的素数，其中产生的分裂度满足 $\log_2N\approx\log_2p$，分布形状在整个范围内稳定，因此检测到的 $(N,N)$-同源度约为 $p^{2}$；观察到的最小最大素幂因子在 $250$ 比特以下保持小，从 $300$ 比特起急剧增加。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the detectability of split surfaces in isogeny graphs of principally polarized superspecial abelian surfaces, a question relevant to the security analysis of dimension-$2$ isogeny-based cryptography. Our approach uses refined Humbert invariants to replace explicit isogeny computations with primitive representation problems for positive definite quadratic forms in five variables. We develop algorithms to detect $(N,N)$-splittings and to compute the minimum $(N,N)$-splitting level of a superspecial Jacobian without constructing the corresponding isogeny path. The same framework detects embeddings of real multiplication (RM) orders through primitive representations of discriminants of real quadratic orders. For RM, we use exhaustive small-prime data together with $100{,}000$ random polarizations per prime for $227\leq p\leq1619$, testing primitive representations of square-free discriminants $D\leq100$; the first nontrivial primitively represented discriminant is typically small. We apply these methods experimentally in two regimes. For $11\leq p\leq251$, where the irreducible principal polarizations are known exhaustively, the automorphism data recovered from the refined Humbert invariant reproduces the counts of Ibukiyama--Katsura--Oort for the irreducible polarizations. For large parameters, we reach primes of $1000$ bits, where the splitting degrees produced satisfy $\log_2N\approx\log_2p$ with a distribution whose shape is stable across the whole range, so the detected $(N,N)$-isogeny has degree about $p^{2}$; the smallest observed largest prime-power divisor of $N$ remains small up to $250$ bits and increases sharply from $300$ bits onward.

    🤖 AI 总结

    💡 在 Drinfeld 上半平面定义 Poincaré 级数，研究其收敛性、非零性，并构造显式线性无关族。
    📖 中文摘要

    借鉴 Kurihara 在 Drinfeld 模形式中的构造，我们在 Drinfeld 上半平面 $\Omega$ 上定义 Poincaré 级数。这些级数由与 Bruhat-Tits 树上测地线自然相关的亚纯 $1$-形式的乘积构成。我们在测地线的有限性条件下建立收敛性，在几种情况下验证该条件，并给出所得尖形式非零的充分条件。对于 $GL_2(\mathbb{F}_q[T])$ 的主同余子群 $\Gamma(\mathfrak{n})$，我们通过从 $\Gamma(\mathfrak{n})\backslash\Omega$ 的解析约化的分量提升某些 $k$-形式，构造显式线性无关的 Poincaré 级数族。我们提出关于尖点消失阶的猜想，并通过计算相应的尖点展开证明了显式 Poincaré 级数族的第一个猜想；作为应用，我们得到 Drinfeld 模形式 $h$ 和 $\Delta$ 作为 Poincaré 级数（差一个符号）。最后，对于在 $\infty$ 分裂的 $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ 上四元数代数相关的余紧群，我们证明 Poincaré 级数张成给定权和型的整个模形式空间。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Adapting a construction of Kurihara in the setting of Drinfeld modular forms, we define Poincar\'e series on the Drinfeld half-plane $\Omega$. These series are built from products of meromorphic $1$-forms that are naturally associated to geodesics on the Bruhat-Tits tree. We establish convergence under a finiteness condition on the geodesics, verify this condition in several cases, and give sufficient conditions for the resulting cusp forms to be nonzero. For the principal congruence subgroup $\Gamma(\mathfrak{n})$ of $GL_2(\mathbb{F}_q[T])$, we construct explicit linearly independent families of Poincar\'e series by lifting certain $k$-forms from the components of the analytic reduction of $\Gamma(\mathfrak{n})\backslash\Omega$. We formulate conjectures on the vanishing orders at cusps, and we prove the first of them for an explicit family of Poincar\'e series by computing the corresponding expansions at the cusps; as an application, we obtain the Drinfeld modular forms $h$ and $\Delta$ as Poincar\'e series (up to a sign). Finally, for cocompact groups attached to quaternion algebras over $\mathbb{F}_q(T)$ that split at $\infty$, we show that the Poincar\'e series span the whole space of modular forms of given weight and type.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立了一种研究任意 $2$-幂次数虚非 CM 域的新方法。特别地，我们证明对于次数大于 $16$，二面体域要么位于三个虚二次域 $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-2})$、$\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})$、$\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-67})$ 之一的射线类域中，该射线类域的导子仅由整除 $2$ 的素数支撑且相关指数有显式上界，要么是虚二次域的 Hilbert 类域。我们还完全分类了 $104$ 个次数 $16$ 类数为 $1$ 的虚非 CM 域，提供了定义多项式、Galois 群、基域和导子的显式表格。这 $104$ 个非 CM 域，连同 Louboutin 和 Okazaki 分类的五个 CM 域，完成了所有次数 $16$ 类数为 $1$ 的虚域的分类。对于 CM 域，问题归结为通过解析类数公式计算相对类数，而对于非 CM 域，缺少指数 $2$ 的完全实子域需要进一步方法，并导致更丰富的 Galois 群和分歧模式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish a new method for studying imaginary non-CM fields of arbitrary \(2\)-power degree. In particular, we show that for degrees greater than \(16\), dihedral fields either lie in the ray class field of one of the three imaginary quadratic fields \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-2})\), \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})\), \(\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-67})\), with the conductor of this ray class field supported only on primes dividing \(2\) and with explicit upper bounds on the relevant exponents, or are Hilbert class fields of imaginary quadratic fields. We also give a complete classification of the \(104\) imaginary non-CM fields of degree \(16\) with class number one, providing explicit tables of defining polynomials, Galois groups, base fields, and conductors. These 104 non-CM fields, together with the five CM fields classified by Louboutin and Okazaki \cite{lou7}, complete the classification of all imaginary fields of degree 16 with class number one. For CM fields, the problem reduces to computing relative class numbers via analytic class number formulae, whereas for non-CM fields the absence of a totally real subfield of index \(2\) necessitates a further approach and leads to a richer variety of Galois groups and ramification patterns.

    🌐 English Abstract

    Uncu and Zudilin studied reflections of finite forms of the modulo nine Kanade--Russell identities under $q\mapsto q^{-1}$, leading to ten conjectural sum--product evaluations, including two proposed by Warnaar. By constructing finite forms of Hickerson's sums and studying their reflections, Konenkov proposed two additional product identities, whose conjugates give two more. In this paper, we prove all eighteen conjectural identities by combining Nahm-sum dual evaluations established by the author with parameterized divided-difference identities. For the symmetric reflections, Schur-type decompositions and finite rational identities reduce the required evaluations to nonsingular two-by-two linear systems.     If $f$ is a tuple of functions satisfying an algebraic ODE and $P\in{\mathbb C}(x)[f]$, it is common in applications to transcendental number theory to consider upper bounds for the order of zero of $P(x,f)$ at a given point in terms of $\operatorname{deg}_x P,\operatorname{deg}_f P$. There are many examples which satisfy our condition $k>2\max\limits_{P\in\mathrm{Per}(f_\infty)}e_{f_{\infty}}(P)$. Indeed, we prove that for every $d\geq2$, a general endomorphism $f_{\infty}$ of $H_{\infty}\cong\mathbb{P}_{\mathbb{C}}^{N-1}$ of degree $d$ satisfies $\max\limits_{P\in H_{\infty}(\mathbb{C})}e_{f_{\infty}}(P)\leq(N-1)!\cdot2^{N-1}$. So if we take $k=(N-1)!\cdot2^N+1$, then $f$ will satisfy our condition if $f_{\infty}$ is general (of an arbitrary degree $d\geq k$). Moreover, we provide examples to illustrate that this condition is optimal to force every periodic curve to be a vertical line, in the sense that one cannot change "$>$" into "$\geq$".

    💡 （未生成总结）
    🌐 English Abstract

    We unify two matching theories, one for finite subsets of groups and the other for finite-dimensional subspaces in a field extension.
